Comparing GAU public news against IC intelligence, she snorted in amusement: GAU media claimed only 15 million lost on the Orion front instead of the true 39.2 million, and dismissed the septillion-killing Dark Matter explosion inside GAU space as a "natural phenomenon" rather than admitting IC's superweapon had hit them. Finally, the PF and Empire war against the GAU sat paused after 400 million losses—mostly PF—amid Arcadian pressure and GAU's economic strain.

Turning the screen toward Dr. Arc, SumireV shook her head. "Another day, another GAU bullsh*t lie. They just can't admit losing to IC even after all their sanctions and blockades failed to breach our borders."

"Well, Sumi," Dr. Arc chuckled, "they are the Top 1 Military—of course they'll spin it." Shaking her head again, SumireV settled back with a cup of coffee, watching TV and trading jokes with Dr. Arc and the base AIs.

Hours later, Nagasawa hurried into the office with an urgent briefing. "SumireV, we have a problem. The Arcadian Army has breached Zenith Triarch territory—their first blow hit the Bureau Coalition border, though Saifuru was on duty and held them back. Saifuru reports the Arcadians run on SolarBurst Energy—sunlight and starlight supercharge their energy and strength, those Solar-Metal Rods drain targets' energy straight back to them, and they draw power directly from their own legions rather than Energy Beacons. Saifuru also learned Order of North took heavy hits last night from Arcadian Elites—apparently there are four Lords total, one deployed to each warfront."

Standing up and crossing her arms, SumireV studied the galactic layout. "Just as expected—since Pivot Federation and its alliance anchor the West as a buffer, Zenith Triarch holds the center, and GAU sits on the East while Order of North holds the North and might sustain itself. Good thing they lack Gravity manipulators and stick mostly to Destructor-based attacks. I've already structured our strike teams—let's move."

Heading straight down to Elite Quarters, SumireV barked, "All Elites, assemble down here NOW!" Counting heads as everyone gathered, she assigned four divisions briskly:

"I'll bring at least two of you—Astra and Mao—to engage the main Arcadian Army directly. Second Division will be Prometheus, Dakari, and Ampasy defending the North flank. Third Division—Mikhail, Iflaren, and Nagasawa—handles the Southern flank. Fourth Division—Codling, Klein, and MiRi—stays ready in case the GAU or its allies try anything sneaky. Everyone else remains in New Pantera to run comms and monitoring. Clear?"

Every Elite nodded—and across Universe-26, IC units including undercover outposts suited up with extra energy reserves as SumireV deployed her armada straight toward the massive Arcadian host that had slipped past The Empire and Imperial Phalanx.

Hovering in open space with her armada aligned cleanly behind her, SumireV watched 2 billion Arcadian soldiers march into view led by Aetheris, who halted briefly upon noticing IC had already blotted out surrounding starlight.

"Ah... a clever enemy," Aetheris murmured to himself. "Still, let's test how well they handle my Destructor power."

Tension crackled across the silent void before Aetheris stepped forward face-to-face with SumireV and fired a blinding Destruction Orb. Without blinking, SumireV manifested a tech portal that sent the orb flashing straight back—forcing Aetheris to dodge swiftly—before tanking his follow-up Supernova Beam untouched inside her Infinity Barrier. Instantly conjuring a heavy Railgun, SumireV fired point-blank—blasting Aetheris inside a 150-megaton TNT shockwave that cracked his ancient armor.

"You're tough," Aetheris admitted, feeling a genuine flicker of fear for the first time since facing the ancient hero millennia ago.

"You stepped onto our territory, and now we bring the entirety of our might to bear against you," SumireV replied evenly. "Prepare to face a torrent of energy and technology."

Smirking darkly, Aetheris stepped back toward his legions. "This will be a long war—let's see who emerges victorious." Both commanders returned to their battle lines.

Chapter 41 - Empyrean & Mundane

With a war cry, Aetheris unleashed his legions straight toward Interstellar Chronicle's armada.

Raising her blade calmly, SumireV gave the signal—and IC's entire battle line erupted in blinding salvos of rockets, lasers, and shells that tore huge holes straight through the charging horde. Before surviving Arcadians could celebrate closing the gap, colossal blue beams charged behind the fleet—Dreadnought Railguns firing specialized thermobaric Voltrauma shells that wiped entire formations across 200-meter blast zones.

"Hold the exact rotation!" SumireV commanded sharply. "Don't panic or lose focus—stay locked in!"

IC executed systematic annihilation like clockwork: Mythril Beasts soaking and redirecting incoming fire at the vanguard, backed by Mechatronics, Armor, Light and Mobile Artillery, Heavy Artillery, Super Heavy Artillery, Warships, Dreadnoughts, and Aircraft Carriers; skies choked with attackers, bombers, kamikaze drones, and gunships; orbit ringed by Global Abominations raining Tungsten Rods tipped with cluster bombs or chemical-bioweapons alongside Orbital Annihilation lasers hunting flagships—every line cycling smoothly during reloads.

Enraged at watching his legions shredded effortlessly, Aetheris blasted sweeping Destruction Beams that killed dozens of IC troops before SumireV seized him mid-cast and slammed him straight into a nearby planet. Grabbing SumireV by the throat and hurling her clear away, Aetheris barely braced before SumireV rocketed back to drive his skull into the dirt—forcing him to teleport clear and blast another Destruction Beam that sheared SumireV's arm clean off as she triggered Vulcan-E2 lightning speed, regenerating the limb before hitting the ground.

Igniting SolarFlare, Aetheris blazed bright orange like a living sun, ragdolling SumireV across the ruins—until she dragged him down under sudden gravity. Screaming into Level 2 SolarFlare to overpower the gravity pull, Aetheris charged forward and drove his fist straight through SumireV's torso... only for the body to dissolve as a mere decoy, leaving him surrounded by hundreds of manifested artillery cannons and rocket pods blasting him flat into the ground.

Activating Serum Phaser-XI Phase 7 in a faint blue-white aura, SumireV met Aetheris as he erupted into dazzling Level 3 SolarFlare—clashing across the wasteland like twin streaks of blinding orange and blue-white light. Sweeping his blade to split the planet's crust along a 31-kilometer fault line, Aetheris staggered back beneath a manifested Hammer slam before SumireV invoked her Core:

"Core of Absolute, Gravity and Creation Ability: Duality of Mundane!"

Crushing pockets of localized gravity pinned Aetheris directly beneath raining artillery shells while a vast manifested dome flashed briefly across the entire planet before vanishing.

"I've mapped every inch of this world," SumireV warned coldly. "Now I can overwhelm your senses completely."

Whirling his spear frantically, Aetheris parried manifested armadas, artillery barrages, and swarms of flying melee blades ranging from tiny kitchen knives to heavy polearms.

"This bi*ch's Creation power—it's pure cheating!" Aetheris snapped.

"Not cheating—just my ability," SumireV replied evenly. "Time to end this."

Firing an Orbital Annihilation beam downward, she watched Aetheris erase the laser with Scatter Destruction Beam before raising one arm high: "Feel the power of gods!" Hurling a roaring black hole straight at her, Aetheris froze as SumireV's eyes blazed crimson and answered with a black hole of her own—smirking coldly:

"Prometheus Modulation Black Hole: Enlarge!"

Expanding rapidly in sheer scale without spending a single extra drop of energy, SumireV's singularity swallowed Aetheris's black hole whole—barely missed him as he dodged and slammed SumireV's face into the stone.

Drawing his Hammer of Havoc, Aetheris swung ruthlessly to force close combat. Abandoning long range, SumireV manifested dual Keris daggers—slicing rapidly past his guard despite his swift regeneration—before switching smoothly into a razor-edged whip!

"I rarely fight anyone with a whip—grrahh!" Aetheris howled as razor lashes tore across his face, snapping SumireV's arm cleanly before kicking her away. Healing instantly via Core restoration, SumireV plunged straight back into ferocious hand-to-hand combat—Aetheris's missed fist pulverizing an entire mountain into gravel before SumireV caught him off balance and snapped his knee!

Enraged, Aetheris unleashed a blistering SolarBurst shockwave that stunned SumireV back, soaring at hypersonic speed behind five spinning Destruction Tendrils Beams that shattered her Infinity Barrier cleanly. Before the beams could tear her apart, SumireV blinked straight inside her Dimension of Logic and stepped calmly back out into open air.

"THAT IS UNFAIR!" Aetheris roared furiously. "You already have Creation and Gravity—NOW DIMENSION TOO?! Accept that you're still mortal while I'm an Empyrean!"

"Still getting battered by a human," SumireV shot back, snapping a manifested cage shut around him.

Shattering the cage with Destructor Spikes, Aetheris began building a massive shockwave—only for SumireV to beat him to it: "Gravity Base: Outward Push!" Blasting Aetheris clear across space onto another planet, she manifested a fresh armada to hammer the world under relentless salvos until Aetheris lay bleeding and battered.

Descending calmly with a manifested poleaxe raised for the kill, SumireV stopped as Aetheris caught the shaft—smirking through bloodied teeth: "You forgot about my ability."

Glancing sharply skyward, SumireV saw the Arcadian legions had ignited an Emergency Artificial Sun overhead—supercharging Aetheris just in time for his fist to slam her hurtling across the sky.

Chapter 42 - Rising Sun

Supercharged by the Emergency Artificial Sun, Aetheris felt his energy surge skyward, unleashing multiple Destructor Beams that effortlessly shattered SumireV's manifested armada. Moving at the speed of light, he battered SumireV with rapid strikes.

"I wish I could kill you instantly," Aetheris snarled, "though my ability has a cooldown and your stupid armor won't rip in half barehanded."

"You talk too much, old man," SumireV shot back, shoving him straight toward the Artificial Sun before summoning TON 618 right on top of it—swallowing the entire star whole. Yet Aetheris survived untouched inside a glowing Destructor Bubble.

"Girl, your Black Hole is far more dangerous than my Destructor because of its range," Aetheris admitted warily. "How can a mere human wield this kind of power?"

"I am the Protector of Humanity. Now let's get locked in," SumireV replied evenly. Activating Serum Phase 9—pushing her power while straining her body—she drew energy from nearby beacons she had staged earlier, shattering past her ceiling to summon six Supermassive Black Holes across the battlefield. Movement thickened and time crawled to a sluggish drift as Aetheris glanced nervously at his own limit, capped at SolarBurst Level 5.

SumireV's voice rang cold and clear across space: "I defended the meaning behind this Core of Absolute, and today SumireV of Interstellar Chronicle shall defend the universe from total annihilation! Tremble before the might of the second user's Core of Absolute and Hero of Humanity! My race will stop you here—you're facing the ingenuity of the human race! This legion of humanity shall stand as long as I live. You declared war on my universe; Human Civilization shall bring the entirety of its might to bear against you! And I stand against you as the mighty Guardian of the Human Race. Law and Logic, not the Chaos of Destruction and Rampage—I shall prevail! The human race has fought for every moment of its survival! Our flesh may be weak, yet we refuse to kneel!"

As crushing gravity pressed downward, Aetheris froze in realization. "No way... I know this power. A Core of Absolute designated to slay gods! No wonder I stand no chance... because I used to be a Divine being."

Manifesting colossal mechanical hands to seize him while Aetheris spammed Destructor Beams to hold them off, SumireV opened a private channel straight to MiRi: "Ready the Divine Bullet. It'll kill him, though I have to weaken him first before you fire. Use GS-2!"

Closing into melee through teleportation and SolarBurst dashes, Aetheris found SumireV keeping strictly out of reach while conjuring swarms of distracting blades.

"Feel this: Cognitive Terror of Humanity!" SumireV declared.

Total sensory chaos erupted around Aetheris—point-blank explosions flashing in his face, blinding spotlights, dummy artillery roaring without firing shells, and towering speakers blasting mocking laughter, haunting shrieks, and agonizing screams. Overloaded across every sense, Aetheris unleashed an ultra-massive SolarBurst Shockwave that blew every weapon clear—spotting SumireV slumped unconscious in the dust ahead. Seizing his chance, he unleashed a devastating Destructor Wave... straight through a hollow mud clone!

Looking sharply skyward, Aetheris froze beneath a massive Radical Collapse—IC tech capable of annihilating an entire Solar System. Raising a frantic Destructor Wall, he blocked the direct blast only for scorching thermal radiation to sear straight through his armor while SumireV rained Orbital Annihilation beams down onto him. The instant the Radical Collapse subsided and his Destructor barrier dropped into cooldown, Divine Bullet GS-02 appeared mere centimeters in front of his chest—detonating point-blank, vacuuming everything inward before erupting outward in a cataclysmic blast.

Emerging through the smoke with 78% of his ancient armor shattered, Aetheris stared in disbelief at SumireV's completely intact nano-armor. "What kind of armor is this?!"

"You're too outdated," SumireV replied coolly. "You're strong, relying on old-era tech built around sheer material hardness—whereas this modern era runs on Nanotech."

Spitting blood, Aetheris snarled, "Still not as good as me—a Divine being!"

SumireV shook her head. "I can literally finish you right now—I just unlocked my next Level, allowing Level 3 Singularity Rebuild. Honestly, I expected you to be stronger, yet you lack modern adaptability. Celestial beings fight better, even some Entropies and Nexuses—you barely rank sixth strongest on my list. Time for you to die so I don't have to worry about you anymore."

With a low groan, Aetheris tore open a teleport rift and vanished out of the battle altogether.

"What a coward," SumireV muttered, immediately hailing every ally across space to hunt the fleeing emperor down.

Chapter 43 - Crumbling Front

Aetheris emerged across the southern territory of Zenith Triarch expecting softer resistance—only to find his legions already crumbling beneath Interstellar Chronicle's systematic encirclement. Furious, he unleashed a Bursting Destruction Blaze that erased everything in its path.

Suddenly—BOOM!—a blazing Orbital Annihilation beam slammed straight into him. Spinning around, Aetheris spotted Nagasawa commanding four orbital lasers hammering his position. Raising a Destructor Wall and hurling it straight at her, he snarled in frustration as it crushed nothing more than another decoy.

Before he could blink, a fast-moving cloud of Red Mist slammed straight into his face—searing his lungs with choking acidic aerosol as Iflaren dropped into view under Stage 4 Velocirax-Nyxvirus Cosmic Variant (VNCV), saturating the air with corrosive venom droplets.

Soaring skyward, Aetheris rained Destructor Orbs down onto IC's Southern Army—only for every orb to stall mid-air!

"All of IC, get out of the radius! Iflaren, pull them back!" Mikhail yelled as the IC armada opened relentless fire on Aetheris inside his protective Destructor Bubble.

Spotting Nagasawa directing the fleet below, Aetheris streaked downward at the speed of light, smashing straight through the armada and wrapping a hand around Nagasawa's throat. Just before he could snap her neck, Nagasawa drove an Unstable Serum Phaser straight into his forearm—melting muscle straight off the bone before Aetheris regenerated rapidly and chased her supersonic retreat. "You little sh*t! Thinking you can beat a godlike being like me when you're just a HUMAN with no ability—only tech?!"

Abruptly, his body locked solid under Mikhail's Telekinesis crushing his internal organs from within. Teleporting behind Mikhail point-blank, Aetheris struck out—only to rebound off an energy barrier nearly as tough as SumireV's! Weakened inside by Iflaren's burning nano-virus, pinned by Mikhail, and scorched continuously by Nagasawa's Orbital Annihilations, Aetheris teleported out once more.

Appearing deep behind SumireV's rear line, he rapidly siphoned IC Energy Beacons until his frame blazed bright orange: "Terraformer: Destructor of Archangel!"

Suddenly, his attack sputtered—looking down in horror to realize his right hand was gone! Turning around, he stared in disbelief at Ampasy casually chewing on his severed hand.

Regenerating swiftly, Aetheris lashed out with a furious punch that cracked Ampasy's facial nano-armor.

"Hey—you aren't very NICE to me! You ruined my food," Ampasy growled. "Now I'll fu*k you UP!"

Igniting Serum Phaser-XI Phase 7 paired with Serum Phaser-ESD, Ampasy surged to 670% Percentile Strength in crackling blue bio-electricity—aggressively battering and tossing Aetheris like a ragdoll in blinding melee combat. Swinging his Destruction Halberd cleanly through Ampasy's torso, Aetheris watched the bisected cyborg simply grin and flip him the middle finger right before gravity yanked him clear!

Streaking in at lightspeed, Prometheus cast a supermassive Sagittarius A* black hole, dragging Aetheris's speed to a crawl. "How many gravity manipulators does this stupid faction have?!" Aetheris raged.

"No, the real question you should ask," Dakari spoke calmly from behind him, "is how long before you accept you're already losing and outdated." Spiky vines coiled swiftly across Aetheris's frame, draining his energy rapidly alongside Bloom Pollinated Spores choking his breath. Blasting upward with Spike Destructor, Aetheris tore free from Prometheus's singularity pull and teleported clear.

Collapsing inside his safe zone near The Empire, Aetheris roared, "FIX ME NOW!" Clutching the Core of Manifestation, he bitterly realized his ancient Core only inflated energy reserves and Destruction range—lacking every ounce of SumireV's versatility. Calling 200 billion fresh troops to assault Zenith Triarch from the North—unaware Saifuru guarded the North alongside Orion, Bureau Coalition, and Tectonic Pact—Aetheris coughed up poisoned blood as the Core of Manifestation whispered seductively in his ear: "You can do it! You are a godly being—never lose to humans!" Swallowing the bait of pride, Aetheris marched north.

Chapter 44 - Unexpected Guest

Back aboard her flagship, SumireV checked sector comms across Zenith Triarch. Mikhail reported nearly killing Aetheris by crushing his organs before he teleported; Prometheus confirmed catching him in the rear line enraged and unstable after failing his big blast.

Calculating swiftly, SumireV deduced Aetheris was avoiding strong fronts—having already failed on three of their six borders. Ruling out New Pantera (never scouted) and the GAU line (unfamiliar terrain), she pointed straight toward Saifuru's Northern Sector and instructed MiRi to keep three standby portals open across northern sectors while she waited.

Meanwhile, leading 200 billion troops north into Zenith Triarch, Aetheris muttered darkly that even his Lords were faltering elsewhere and he knew only destruction. When his commander nervously advised falling back to preserve the army, Aetheris snapped furiously: "Shut your damn hole! We ATTACK from the NORTH—there won't be much resistance there! I can't afford losing momentum to inferior human sh*t!" Buoyed by the Core of Manifestation's whispers, Aetheris broke into maniacal laughter.

Hours later, everyone aboard Aetheris's flagship suddenly tasted sharp metal. Radar screamed alerts of an incoming object hurtling at Mach 50—right before Zero of Orion-TW3 Prototype Team Gamma slammed straight into the hull in a 50-megaton TNT explosion!

Dropping out of Transversal Speed Travel, an Orion Armada opened fire as an enraged Aetheris burst outside blasting Destruction Beams—only to shriek in agony as a sudden flash coated his hand in scorching Molten Steel from Matteo raining nails overhead.

"ARTHUR, NEED AN ASSIST!" Matteo shouted.

Amplifying air particles at hypersonic speed via MV, Arthur drove the nails straight through Aetheris's flesh to melt deep inside him. Unleashing a violent Destructor shockwave meant to burst Orion's internal organs, Aetheris watched them drop motionless—assuming them dead.

Slamming his fist onto a control console in throbbing pain, Aetheris confronted his strategic nightmare: stranded deep inside hostile Zenith Triarch (his worst enemy), his elites locked in battle against Order of North across three fronts, having merely slipped past the Attrition Titan Alliance (The Empire, Cluster Creed, Pivot Federation, and Imperial Phalanx) without actually defeating them.

Reaching the destroyed border between Cluster Creed and Bureau Coalition, Aetheris ordered his surviving troops to establish a forward base—completely unaware Order of North had secretly staged Railguns loaded with Anti-Matter Shells in retaliation for Arcadian Elite raids days earlier!

"FIRE!" the Order of North captain barked, tearing devastating laser salvos straight through the Arcadian anchorage before smoothly pulling back out of range—leaving IC and its allies to finish off the battered emperor while Northern defenders focused on Arcadian Elites.

Watching 40% of his army vanish in seconds, Aetheris spun toward humming engines and pulsing lights rising behind the ruined base as IC, Orion, and Bureau Coalition warships unloaded full broadsides!

"We never talk empty—we deliver on promises," Saifuru called out smoothly at the helm. "Remember what my boss SumireV told you? 'We bring the entirety of our might to stand and bear against you.' Unlike you—all big talk and zero proof, loud like an empty can!"

Stung bitterly by the truth, Aetheris braced for impact.

Chapter 45 - Mighty Force of IC

Battered relentlessly beneath Coalition Zenith barrages, Aetheris staggered as Saifuru blitzed across his blind spots at blinding lightning speed. Hiding inside a Destructor Bubble against Coalition artillery, Aetheris unleashed a supermassive Destructor Orb engulfing half the coalition fleet—straining himself heavily in the process.

Unbroken, Saifuru plunged straight through the blast and drove Voltrauma—freezing lightning inducing lethal lung necrosis—directly into Aetheris's chest before surging Serum Phaser-XI Phase 6 through the wound, detonating across the nearby planet below.

Drifting gravely wounded in empty space without a single sun nearby as his armada burned, Aetheris twisted his bloodied face into a dark smile. "You all don't realize... I am a Deity."

Blasting both his fleet and Coalition Zenith outward with a massive shockwave, his body flared brilliantly with SolarBurst! "Thanks to your lightning, Saifuru, I recharged enough energy to awaken my Crystals! Now I, a Deity, will show you what happens when mortals cross us!" Charging at Saifuru, Aetheris still couldn't match his speed—yet Saifuru's strikes suddenly inflicted zero damage! Swinging his Halberd, Aetheris blasted Saifuru back with a devastating SunRay, leaving him critically wounded and forced to retreat while Aetheris shattered everything resisting him with Destructor Shockwaves.

Two hours earlier, learning Aetheris had engaged Orion Team Gamma in the North, SumireV had raced toward Saifuru's front—pausing along the way to conjure medical transports rescuing retreating Bureau Coalition soldiers. Arriving just in time to catch Saifuru spitting blood in the rear lines, she listened to his rapid warning: "He activated his Deity power—immune to damage, instant regeneration, even his armor heals—probably a Core ability!" Healing Saifuru swiftly and ordering him to pull Coalition Zenith back, SumireV raised a Massive Tungsten Barrier to absorb Aetheris's incoming SunBlast, deflecting shells while conjuring evacuation transports as Saifuru sped everyone clear.

Alone at last with the Emperor once the evacuation cleared, SumireV focused Core Vision onto Aetheris—clearly spotting the Core of Manifestation alongside five unknown crystalline objects inside his frame. Determined to probe his strengths and limits carefully, she lowered into a battle stance.

"Let's fight. We end this once and for all."

Chapter 46 - Humanity and Deity

SumireV opened by conjuring an armada unleashing heavy barrages—watching calmly as Aetheris absorbed the incoming blasts effortlessly. Narrowly evading a colossal Spiral Destructor using Vulcan-E2 Voltrauma lightning speed and Serum Phaser-XI Phase 8, SumireV immediately manifested six Railguns packing 50 megatons of TNT apiece and fired—spotting the crucial flaw the moment his armor cracked: Aetheris absorbed elemental damage, yet couldn't shrug off massive physical impact!

Drawing surging power from her Mini Nuclear Fission Reactors (MNFR), SumireV dropped a point-blank black hole ripping strips off his armor, erected a Dome of Logic mapping every meter of open space for precision spawns, and anchored Phoenix A in the center—dragging time down and weighting Aetheris's movements while proclaiming humanity's defiance:

"I am the Embodiment of Humanity! SumireV of Interstellar Chronicle shall defend Universe-26 from the destructive force of the Arcadian Army! My race will stop you here—you're facing the ingenuity of the human race! This legion of humanity shall stand as long as I live! You declared war on my universe; Human Civilization shall bring the entirety of its might to bear against you!"

Flooding the arena with thousands of regenerating warships while forcing errors with gravity, SumireV unleashed Gravitational Fracture—warping gravity violently left and right in chaotic shears that stripped away most of his armor, exposing the glowing Crystals inside.

Enraged, Aetheris erupted into SolarBurst Level 5, flooding space with curving Destructor Beams shaped into shields, tendrils, and razor lasers simultaneously. As cracks splintered her Infinity Barrier, SumireV stepped smoothly inside her Dimension of Logic using Dimensional ability and stepped cleanly right back out!

"THAT IS UNFAIR!" Aetheris bellowed. "First Creation, then Gravity, NOW DIMENSION?!"

"That's how I work," SumireV replied coolly.

Drained from spamming beams, Aetheris tapped his Crystals for rapid recovery—right before SumireV manifested a million rifles firing Iflaren's Vile Venom Bullets, overwhelming his pain receptors. Adapting rapidly with sudden immunity to railguns and lasers, Aetheris smirked—only for SumireV to swap instantly to Vulcan-E2 Molten Lava, melting straight through his physical guard before flash-freezing the slag with Ice Icicles to lock his limbs in agonizing stone!

Shattering free with Spikey Destructor, Aetheris blitzed at lightspeed, slammed SumireV crashing into a nearby planet, and charged a lethal Destructor Wave—only for SumireV to slam him backward with a manifested bulldozer and bury him beneath 50 Nuclear Warhead Rockets blasting a massive crater across the surface!

"And I stand against you as a mighty Guardian of the Human Race!" SumireV declared cleanly. "Law and Logic, not the Chaos of Destruction and Death—I shall prevail! The human race has fought for every moment of its survival! Our flesh may be weak, yet we refuse to kneel!"

Pushing straight past her old ceiling at last, SumireV fully unlocked Level 3 Core of Absolute mastery.

Chapter 47 - Human Terror

SumireV immediately created two missiles tipped with Velocirax-Nyxvirus Warheads. "As long as energy exists here, I can manipulate it using the Core of Absolute to manifest anything I understand and know how it works."

Aetheris fired a Destructor Laser that blasted both missiles apart mid-air—completely missing the real threat overhead. High in orbit, Global Abomination launched a Tungsten Rod carrying a Velocirax-Nyxvirus Warhead straight down onto Aetheris, stunning and poisoning him on impact! Seeing him weakened, SumireV rushed into aggressive close combat, manifesting four Giant Mechanical Hands to pin him fast before forming a miniature black hole across her palm and pressing it directly against his exposed Core and Crystals—slowly digesting them!

Panicking, Aetheris tore loose and bolted away at lightspeed while SumireV gave chase at lightning thunder speed. Unable to match lightspeed normally, she boosted her velocity through a Gravity Tunnel, spawned TON 618 directly ahead of Aetheris to throw off his path, closed within 15 kilometers, and snapped Clock-Lock—freezing localized time across a 30-kilometer radius. Certain she had him cornered to crush into TON 618—KABOOM!

Knocked violently backward, SumireV stared in shock as Aetheris overloaded himself with Natural Energy, instantly dropping ambient energy levels around them to 0%. Firing up her second MNFR, SumireV heard his desperate roar:

"With this Core of Manifestation, I will detonate myself alongside Universe-26! No one wins—ONLY DEATH AND DESTRUCTION!"

SumireV tried crushing him with gravity to no avail, dropped TON 618 directly atop him only for a Total Destructor Bubble ringed by spinning tendrils to repel everything closing in, and hammered him with every weapon in IC's arsenal through Creation—watching massive explosions wash harmlessly over his shield. Switching tactics rapidly, she alternated elemental strikes to bleed his energy reserves with physical rockets and artillery to shear off his tendrils. It worked, yet too slowly—barely three minutes remained until detonation, and Clock-Lock slowed only the surrounding space rather than him.

"I have to end you my own way," SumireV whispered. "Hopefully this works."

Her eyes glowing deep blue, SumireV activated her Dimensional Ability and enveloped Aetheris inside a Pocket Dimension. Trapped inside, Aetheris stared in sudden terror as his Destructor Energy froze completely dormant—high yet locked unable to climb or drop. SumireV's eyes blazed bright crimson as she invoked Gravity Ability:

"Core of Absolute, Gravity Ability... 3rd Level: Singularity Rebuild—Infinity Deconstruction!"

"NOOOO!!!" Aetheris shrieked, instantly erased from the system—leaving only the Core of Manifestation clattering onto the ground. Retrieving the Core, SumireV broadcast confirmation across Coalition Zenith that Aetheris had been permanently eliminated, then opened a portal inside her Dimension straight back to New Pantera.

Drained to her core, SumireV met Crown upon arrival. "Guard New Pantera—I'm heading into my Chamber to rest."

Retreating into her Healing Chamber—where restorative particles saturated the air, calming verses played gently over radio to mend spiritual strain, and Natural Energy generators restored lost reserves—SumireV watched news broadcasts confirm Zenith Triarch had crushed the Arcadian Army within its borders. Across deep space, The Attrition Titan slowly pushed back remaining Arcadians, The Polar Starborne Alliance mobilized full armadas to counterattack Arcadian Elite Forces, and Supreme Administrative Bloc leader GAU sat sidelined by civil protests and division (leaving Parliament Abstract alone to reinforce Order of North with troops).

Resting quietly, SumireV reflected that Aetheris had been formidable, yet doomed by his limits, heavily outdated tech, and the subtle corruption clinging to his Forbidden Destructor Havoc.

Chapter 48 - Cleaning and Sanitizing

Three days later, SumireV rejoined IC's Elites to purge the aftermath of war across Zenith Triarch. Arriving in the Southern Sector where scattered Arcadian remnants had mutated under the Nano-Virus, SumireV stepped outside her ship, manifested dozens of battleships to gun down the hordes, and gestured for Codling to finish sanitizing the world. Moving steadily from planet to planet alongside 500 billion Orion-TW3 soldiers deployed to assist cleanup, IC restored order rapidly.

Meanwhile, the Northern Sector fell to Iflaren, Ampasy, Saifuru, MiRi, and Crown backed by Tectonic Pact and Bureau Coalition crews—facing the heaviest devastation from SumireV's clash with Aetheris. Orion-TW3 spread across Central, South, and West while fortifying Western and Northern borders against Arcadian armies still battling external factions. Back in New Pantera, Devin, Nagasawa, Astra, and Klein coordinated logistics and portal gates, while Dakari worked nonstop in the Central Sector growing giant Oak hospitals and spraying healing spores across endless waves of sick and wounded soldiers.

Flying out to the Western Border to inspect Orion-TW3 units—who needed a protective perimeter installed while their transport ships evacuated casualties—SumireV borrowed one of Orion's nuclear energy reactors, absorbed its power, and constructed a far denser, hyper-realistic physical energy barrier all along the western frontier, watching distant flashes flare from The Empire and Imperial Phalanx fronts. Across the Northern Section, Saifuru wove an identical barrier at blinding speed using Voltrauma.

Out in the Southwest, Prometheus and Mikhail hunted down the final retreating Arcadian pockets—Mikhail locking elites down so they couldn't move or cast while Prometheus swallowed them inside black holes, Mikhail simultaneously halting incoming SolarBurst lasers and crushing enemies' internal organs via telekinesis.

"Behind you, Prome!" Mikhail yelled—just before BOOM! Crown dropped cleanly into the middle with a Repulsive-Force blast disintegrating the ambushers!

"Good thing I came to assist you both," Crown grinned. "Just wanted to see how it felt fighting side by side with you two!"

"You're the rookie who ran his first mission against Pivot Federation months ago and won—you're exceptional!" Mikhail praised.

"Hey, quit chatting and help me finish these guys faster so I can go play video games at base!" Prometheus grumbled.

Leaping into the enemy formation, Crown unleashed a massive Repulsive Force blast across a 350-kilometer radius while Mikhail crushed Herald elites and Prometheus's Sagittarius A* vaporized the final fugitives fleeing south. Reporting cleanup complete over comms ("Captain Sumi, we cleared the last Arcadian pocket in Zenith Triarch!"), Prometheus and his team received immediate orders from SumireV to hurry back to base for a full briefing.

Chapter 49 - Aid Incoming

Waiting inside the assembly hall as IC's Elites filed in one by one, SumireV waited until Prometheus, Mikhail, and Crown took their seats before speaking briskly.

"Come closer, everyone. Our next move is assisting The Attrition Titan—starting Northwest by entering Cluster Creed. The objectives are straightforward. First, kill any Arcadian Army forces on sight. Second, locate and rescue civilians or unarmed Attrition Titan soldiers. Third, collect dead bodies to bury or burn them. Fourth, sanitize all spores. Clear? Any questions, speak up now."

"What about the Pivot Federation?" Klein raised her hand cautiously. "We fought a war with them four months ago—they definitely still hold a grudge against IC."

"And I used to be an Empire scientist," Iflaren added. "They know who I am, and we just had a border clash weeks before the Arcadians showed up!"

SumireV paused silently before replying. "First, broadcast that we're operating as peacekeepers and avoid engaging them as best you can. HOWEVER, if they attack you—just kill them. I want this mission wrapped up as quickly as possible. Gear up; you all have two hours before launch."

Heading to the Control Room with Dr. Arc, Dr. Edward, and MiRi, SumireV pulled up the latest maps.

"Give me the update on The Attrition Titan—focusing on Cluster Creed," she ordered.

"There are four surviving Pocket Resistances still holding against the Arcadians," Dr. Arc reported. "Most civilians already fled into Imperial Phalanx and The Empire. That said, things shifted recently—Cluster Creed just deployed their Paladin Force, which can manipulate reality in limited cases. Imperial Phalanx, The Empire, and Pivot Federation are trying to push inside Cluster Creed too, though all three remain pinned down defending their own solar systems."

"Alright, at least we know where Attrition Titan's forces stand," SumireV nodded. "Dr. Edward, any recommendations for our loadout?"

"Sumi, I suggest packing Nano-Virus Canisters—and I'd like to deploy two Divisions just in case we run into Cosmic Entities."

"Approved—I trust your judgment. MiRi, arrange our armada and apply whatever modifications you see fit."

Two hours later, the Interstellar Chronicle Cluster Creed Campaign (IC^4) launched across three distinct groups—Breacher, Collector, and Sanitizer—with 5,000 vessels per group plus spare Energy Cells. Five hours out, crossing the border past slaughtered Cluster Creed refugees (whose massacre had drawn local Bureau Coalition outposts into skirmishes with Arcadian raiders), IC^4 pressed relentlessly inward.

Seventeen hours later, IC^4 arrived outside the first Cluster Creed Pocket Defense clinging on behind glowing Paladin barriers.

"All units—wipe out those Arcadians," SumireV commanded.

IC's Breacher Group opened up with thunderous artillery and Railgun barrages while Mikhail pinned down an incoming Arcadian cosmic unit so MiRi's armor-stripping missiles could tear it apart. Perimeter cleared, SumireV descended onto the field as the Paladin shield lowered—facing cautious, mixed glares from surviving Cluster Creed soldiers as she stepped before Vihaan.

"SumireV of Interstellar Chronicle," Vihaan spoke evenly. "What brings you inside my territory?"

"We're here to help clear out the Arcadian Army. Because—"

"I know you defeated Aetheris," Vihaan cut in quietly.

SumireV paused briefly. "Then you know we can help liberate your systems—nothing needed in return unless you choose to offer it. Arcadians are a far worse threat than I am, and once they realize defeat is near, they'll push for maximum destruction across your worlds."

Turning slightly, Vihaan exchanged quiet whispers with his Elite guard—who nodded grimly given how desperate the war stood—before extending a hand.

"Cluster Creed will work alongside Interstellar Chronicle." Together, the two leaders shook hands.

Chapter 50 - Unexpected Turn

Fighting shoulder to shoulder, Interstellar Chronicle and Cluster Creed swept swiftly through besieged sectors—IC Special Elites coordinating smoothly with CC Paladin Elites, both matching Nexus-level techniques at Sentinel-tier minimum. SumireV and Vihaan hunted down Arcadian breeding Hives personally, torching them cleanly with low-cost flame strikes before splitting to clear underground metros and mall basements.

Across adjacent districts, Codling and Devin ran brutal portal traps—Devin opening sudden doorways straight into enemy bunkers ("CODLING, THROW THE IMPLOSION BOMB!") before snapping portals shut, allowing Nagasawa to pinpoint every blast site and drop two-stage Napalm Thermobaric bombs. Over on the spore-choked east side, where a skeptical CC guard insisted clearing the ground-zero infestation was impossible, Iflaren flooded the entire sector in thick crimson Red Mist that melted every spore cleanly with acid—cheerfully advising pale-faced Cluster Creed soldiers (who remembered Sigil Compact horrors all too well) not to breathe it in.

Advancing toward their second target city alongside IC elites, SumireV froze abruptly as space warped and time dragged thick around them.

"STOP! ALL OF YOU, FALL BACK!" SumireV roared!

Out of thin air descended a Celestial Council ambush squad: "COME ON—GET THEM!"

Instantly conjuring Quasi Black Holes, De-Constructing approaching foes, and launching nine Anti-Celestial Missiles that drew Celestial blood, SumireV fought beside Prometheus's medium Sagittarius A* and Ampasy leaping onto a Celestial's throat—until the ambushers pooled their strength in a split second, restraining SumireV and teleporting her clean across the multiverse while locking Universe-26 out completely!

"FALL BACK—WE CAN'T FIGHT ALL OF IC!" the battered Celestials gasped, retreating swiftly as Ampasy finished ripping one Celestial apart and bellowed in fury: "FUK, FUK, FU*K! THEY GOT SUMIREV! WE DON'T HAVE OUR LEADER!"

Hailing MiRi frantically over comms in New Pantera—"Reporting—this is Prometheus! SumireV is gone, teleported somewhere unknown! We need a temporary leader NOW!"—IC pulled every panicked unit safely back through Devin's emergency portals.

Stranded across an unfamiliar void, SumireV spotted a spacefaring civilization calling itself Alvitr Zeta—advanced, though nowhere near IC tech. Finding comms dead, she slipped inside Dimension of Logic and commanded, "Doorway to Universe-26," only to slam against an empty wall as if Universe-26 didn't exist. Stepping back into real space and approaching Alvitr Zeta's perimeter, she was halted by armed guards informing her she stood in Universe-4510 amid a desperate war against Black Pandora—prompting SumireV to demand an audience with their leader immediately.

Meanwhile, inside Celestial Council chambers, the Omnipotence Celestial demanded results.

"We failed to seal all IC elites—SumireV activated her powers and drained our energy badly," the strike commander reported. "Still, we combined strength in a split second to teleport her to a distant universe while Atlas locked Universe-26 out of her Tesseract access—tough work, according to Atlas."

"That's good enough," the Omnipotence Celestial smiled smoothly. "Now we don't have to worry—SumireV is gone, and those IC subordinates don't hold Cores like hers, so we're safe." Counting their fallen members, the Council set to recharging their nullified reserves.

Chapter 51 - Interstellar Chronicle

Seven hours after SumireV vanished, tense silence gripped the IC Meeting Room—even Dr. Arc's holographic features looked strained.

"SO WHAT NOW?!" Ampasy shouted furiously. "We need a new leader! Who here is even close to SumireV?!"

"God... I'm just an engineer," MiRi whispered helplessly.

Crossing his arms silently, Iflaren watched his comrades ("I left The Empire and finally found a real family here—we cannot let IC fall"), while Saifuru anxiously ran contingency plans through base AI.

Suddenly, Devin stood up and pointed straight at Codling. "I think he knows what to do! Codling thinks almost the exact same way SumireV does!"

Ampasy spun on Devin. "Are you stupid?! This guy's practically brand new to IC—I STILL don't understand why SumireV even made him an Elite!"

"Stop," Astra cut Ampasy off firmly. "Codling is ridiculously adaptive and sharp. I worked with him during the GAU Campaign in Orion-TW3, he cleared southern Zenith Triarch, followed SumireV into the Honduran Path, and stood with us in Cluster Creed—deployed over three times in just two months! Do you not see the pattern?"

Murmurs swept the table until Codling stood quietly to his feet, silencing the entire room.

"Look—we need to pull together!" Codling spoke steadily. "Even without SumireV, Interstellar Chronicle is ONE. Relying solely on SumireV left our structure fragile—one single pillar holding up everything from collapse. So we split that weight across multiple pillars, unified under one creed: We are humanity, fighting for every moment of our survival! Our flesh may be weak, yet we refuse to kneel to Cosmic Entities or Celestials! Fear is Instinct, Courage is Humanity!"

"What's the plan, New Boss?" Nagasawa asked softly, leaning forward.

"First, we define our Pillars," Codling answered calmly. "IC runs across departments: MiRi as Head of Engineering; Dr. Edward as Head of Biochemical backed by Iflaren; Dr. Pedro as Head of Energy backed by Saifuru; Nagasawa leading Communication and Strategy; and Dr. Zack heading Divine Bullet and Rockets backed by Ampasy—a cyborg strong enough to fight Celestials! Those are IC's Pillars. Every department focuses on production first. Once we stabilize and lock down our homefront, then we relieve Zenith Triarch and The Attrition Titan. My mother always said, 'Try clothes on yourself first'—know your own limits before reaching out to save everyone else!"

One by one, the Elites stood and applauded—some cheering "Codling is IC's leader!" while Klein beamed warmly: "Come on, Cody—we're all with you! Our Hero! Our Protector!"

Awake alone in his quarters that night, Codling opened a drawer and took out a voice recorder SumireV had handed him days before the Celestial ambush. Pressing play, he heard SumireV's calm voice:

"Codling... I see something rare in you—something enemies fear and covet. Your Petrify skill is strong, yet it's your mind that caught my eye, especially your quote: 'Humanity is weak, yet we are bestowed with Mind—and Mind is a powerful place capable of creating good or destruction.' Bear with me, Codling—I know you can sense something huge watching me, and I might not always be around. I love IC dearly and want it protected. If I vanish, I left something behind this recorder—press the back panel. It's a gift designed and tested 100% by me. It grants extra energy reserves, sharper cognition, and Creation-base power like my Core of Absolute—allowing you to manifest anything whose mechanics and concept you understand. I left out Gravity and Dimension so it won't burn your Core out too fast. Your mind is your compass. Good luck."

Shaking the silent casing with nothing happening at first, Codling breathed quietly: "Humanity's symbolic cognition, creativity, instinct, and adaptability..."

Crack! The back panel split open, revealing a crimson-red Core labeled Core of Diversity Potential—which suddenly blazed bright red and melted straight through his sternum into his heart, dropping him unconscious in searing agony!

Waking next day in the medical bay to find Klein leaning anxiously beside his bed ("Am I dead? Why am I seeing an angel?" — "Oh my gosh, are you okay, Codling?!"), he ripped back the blanket to find smooth, unscarred skin over his chest. Running straight outside, he pointed at the open field: "Black Hole Emerge!" Nothing appeared—yet power surged strongly inside him.

"Klein! Create me a book about gravity!" he asked quickly.

"Um, I don't know much about gravity—only military war machines and tech."

Grabbing her shoulders urgently, Codling pleaded, "Do it anyway—I need to test something! Give me a manual!"

Shoving him gently with a laugh, Klein manifested a thick manual on artillery mechanics using Synthetic Mirage. Scanning every schematic intensely for five minutes, Codling locked onto the empty range:

"Artillery! High-explosive shells, 155mm—ignition, rapid-fire gas expansion, hydraulic pneumatic recoil!"

Ambient energy snapped into solid steel, roaring across the field in a thunderous blast!

"YES!! YES!! I AM THE PROTECTOR OF HUMANITY!!" Codling cheered, grabbing Klein's hands excitedly. "I need to train! Take me to the Institute—I know you love and have a crush on me, and I feel the exact same way about you, now help me study!" Blushing and smiling, Klein manifested a sleek synthetic jet and flew him off toward the Institute of Aviation Engineering—just moments before Crown and Nagasawa reached the empty medical bay looking for him.

Chapter 52 - Long Journey

Inside the Alvitr Zeta Command Center, SumireV stood under sharp interrogation by Commander Nate.

"Let me meet your King," SumireV demanded evenly.

"No—you're completely unknown to us," Nate snapped. "We have zero records of you! Besides, we're literally in a warzone right now, and you drop out of nowhere demanding to meet our leader?! Nonsense! You must be some foolish youngster pulling a prank!"

Before Nate finished speaking, Black Pandora's assault fleet struck—lasers slamming straight through Alvitr's orbital ships before defenses could react. Knocked bleeding across the floor, Nate crawled frantically toward his armor suit as Pandora Special Forces breached the Command Center firing at everyone in sight. Spotting Nate, a Pandora trooper raised his rifle—only for SumireV to yank the squad inward under supersonic gravity, vaporizing them on the spot.

Stepping outside into the shattered street, SumireV saw Alvitr defenders breaking down as a towering Pandora cosmic entity descended from orbit—weeping in despair, "They deployed a god!"

Manifesting four heavy Artillery pieces loaded with Explosive Molten Shells, SumireV opened fire, making the Herald-tier entity howl in pain before she manifested six Supercharged Railguns that tore it apart inside roaring 20-megaton TNT explosions.

Spotting ten more enemy Divisions closing from the north, SumireV's eyes blazed bright white.

"Core Absolute, Creation Base: Reconstruction of Interstellar Chronicle Arsenal!"

Every weapon in IC's history materialized behind her—from compact handguns all the way up to Global Abomination orbital strikes—unleashing a deafening deluge laced with nano-virus shells. Even enemy Heralds couldn't endure the storm; hearing an enemy commander shout "We can't lose our Goliaths—bring them back!", SumireV pressed relentlessly as Pandora retreated behind protective energy shields. Aiming upward, she manifested Divine Bullet GS-01—"Thank goodness this universe has Natural Energy; built to kill Entropy and beings above Sentinels"—and fired, watching the warhead erupt across Pandora's fleet before imploding and scattering debris outward.

Stunned Alvitr soldiers stared after her in whispered awe ("Is she an Upper God?" "A Higher Authority?!") while SumireV manifested a clean emergency field hospital to tend Nate's wounds.

"Now is that enough proof to meet your leader?" she asked gently, handing him water.

Too overwhelmed to speak, Nate nodded gratefully with his eyes—resting quietly as SumireV stood guard over the ruined skyline in case Black Pandora returned.

Chapter 53 - Previous Era Absolution

Eager to get home to Universe-26, SumireV asked around Alvitr Zeta—only to confirm their Type 1.2 Civilization lacked inter-universal travel tech (far behind IC's Type 3). Storming the Celestial realm directly seemed far too risky. Remembering Julian's parting hint, she flew out into quiet deep space and gripped her Core of Absolute—allowing the blinding light to pull her straight inside the Realm of Absolute.

Opening her eyes across the ash-covered plains amid quiet, distant predecessors, SumireV walked over to Alan.

"Hello, successor! We know why you're here," Alan greeted warmly.

"Look, guys—those Celestials teleported me far out after I killed a few of them," SumireV explained. "I need tech to get back to Universe-26 without fighting through Celestials head-on."

"We know a genius," Victoria smiled, pointing upward toward Ethan—the 262nd user—meditating high in the sky. Flying up beside him, SumireV watched Ethan snap out of meditation immediately: "My granddaughter!! I missed you!"

"Hey, Ethan—I just need your help—"

"Shh, I know. Follow me."

Inside Ethan's sprawling Type 5–6 laboratory—lined with variants of Prometheus Modulation, pointing out her own balanced Prometheus Modulation Aegis-101 (PMA-101)—Ethan examined the problem for hours before admitting his drive only mapped universes he had personally visited, suggesting Eric (the 234th user) instead. Scanning with Core Vision to spot Eric across the Realm, SumireV barely launched at hypersonic speed before Eric snatched her straight back into Ethan's lab at lightspeed!

"No need for that, successor—I can hear just fine. I'll help," Eric said briskly, squinting at Ethan's console. "You sure this'll work? You rely too much on hardware without mastering Spatial Quantum."

"Shh! Don't make me lose my cool in front of SumireV!" Ethan hissed, before suggesting they build a brand-new travel device together—with Zimmerman's help transferring concepts back into reality—promptly shooing SumireV outside their research terminal.

Wandering Ethan's Compound, SumireV bumped into Ragna (the 261st user), who happily offered to guide her east toward the Realm's most advanced Simulator. Passing glowing historical records of predecessors' final stands—Ethan wiping Celestial fleets with Quad Black Holes, Alan battling multiversal monsters, Ronan right before the Liminal attack, and Oslo (the 30th user) clashing with Higher Authorities using Principal Ingenuity—they reached the Simulator.

Stepping inside, SumireV picked Ethan first:

Opening with an armada barrage, SumireV watched Ethan reflect every shell with gravity, blur at lightspeed, and vaporize her with a Gamma Ray Beam in just two minutes and thirty seconds! Restarting immediately, she summoned TON 618—only for Ethan to erase it with a counter-spinning Reverse Black Hole, deflect Mindstorm, blind her inside a Blinding Black Hole, and impale her with a black-hole spear at seven minutes and forty-five seconds.

Switching next to Valket (the 96th user), she suffered instant Game Over twice in two seconds—Ragna explaining she lacked the Cosmic Resilience simply to withstand Valket's suppressive Cosmic Control. Choosing Ragna third, SumireV felt sick from suit-piercing Epsilon Rays, watched her 3rd-Level Singularity Rebuild frozen down to zero by 4th-Level Absolute Zero, and fell inside Ragna's superior Dimension of Logic at five minutes—earning applause since newcomers normally lasted barely thirty seconds.

Resting in the cafeteria afterward over holo-footage of Valket splitting eight universes with Elysian of Decimation, SumireV stared in humbled awe beside ancestors who had evolved into Sentinels while she stayed mortal—watching how each mastered Physics pillars (Space, Time, Gravity, Thermodynamics, Acoustics, Quantum, Dimensional, Creation, Electromagnetism) in their own signature style.

"Ragna," SumireV asked quietly, "can you tell me the full history of the Core of Absolute? Why only certain people are chosen—and how everyone ended?"

Ragna exhaled slowly. "Ernst—our first wielder and inventor, who reached Level 20 while you're at 3 approaching 4—forged the Core rather than finding it naturally. Humanity kept getting oppressed and harvested by cosmic and divine beings, so he built a weapon specifically to fight back. Chosen wielders share fierce cognition, courage, determination, and adherence to Logic and Law over chaos—remember: Fear is Instinct, Courage is Humanity.

"As for our endings... they're tragic. Either the Core eventually rejects an aging user—capping your energy and making beloved abilities drain instead of spam until you let go—or you fall in battle. Valket died fighting two Omega-Class Higher Authorities, sealing 90 universes behind his dying Divinity Lock. Ethan turned himself into a black hole sealing Prometheus Modulation. Oslo succumbed to corruption at Level 9 while forcing Level 11 and 13 strikes against Deities, leaving glitched, damaged sectors scarred across the multiverse. As for me, I hailed from Universe-9 and died of old age once restoration faded. And Ernst? Zimmerman said he vanished from the Realm when the 50th user arrived—rumored to have returned to reality as a Type-8 pioneer, though only the 20th through 50th wielders fighting Catalist Imperial might know for sure."

Meanwhile, back inside Universe-26's Golden Memory Archive, Crown and Devin stood frozen before the floating projection—watching SumireV alive inside the Realm of Absolute while predecessors cleaved universes apart!

"SUMI!! CAN YOU HEAR US?!" Devin yelled frantically at the glass before bolting out with Crown to alert the other Elites.

Chapter 54 - Imperfection

Inside the Institute of Weaponry library, Codling studied technical manuals intently with Klein guiding him. Attempting to manifest an IC space battleship outside, he watched the complex hull melt and explode in seconds.

"You've only had the Core for three days, Cody," Klein reassured gently. "Mastery doesn't happen overnight—it takes months or years. Besides, IC battleships are vastly complex, whereas you usually focus on energy rather than heavy warships."

Thinking quickly, Codling brightened. "Lead me to the World War II naval warfare museum!"

Stepping aboard the preserved USS Iowa inside the ancient war museum, Codling studied its blueprints and turrets closely for hours—admiring old-school naval firepower—before walking outside onto the test range. Focusing his Core of Diversity Potential, he manifested the towering battleship for ten glorious seconds and ordered a full broadside—shattering the quiet air with roaring 406mm cannons!

"FINALLY! I can mimic SumireV!" Codling cheered, pausing abruptly with newfound respect. "God... imagine how many years SumireV suffered just to master Creation through pure understanding!"

Hurrying next to the aviation hangar, Codling waved off Klein's suggestions of F-35s and F-16s, uncovering simpler, faster-manifesting classics tucked in the back wing: a B-2 Raider, an SR-71 Blackbird, an A-10 Warthog, and a Messerschmitt Me 262—easy to grasp, low energy cost, and ripe for adding modern reactors later.

Just then, Crown and Nagasawa arrived after asking librarians where he went—staring wide-eyed at four battleships and twenty planes sitting outside! Breaking the huge news that they had spotted SumireV alive inside the Golden Archive—stranded in a distant universe—Crown watched Codling sprint straight back toward IC Stronghold.

Chapter 55 - SumireV's Death

High on Serenity Cliff—a secluded, peaceful sanctuary inside Cluster Creed packed with mainland refugees—soldiers listened to distant flashes of war across the horizon, protected so far across five failed Arcadian assaults by hardened veterans from the Sigil Compact era.

Climbing the mountain seeking why their defenders stood so strong, young soldier Drago met an ancient seer alongside Libra—who had Schwarz teleport everyone down into Serenity Cliff's crowded plaza so the refugees could finally hear the unspoken truth.

Stepping forward with steady voice, Libra recounted Azure Unit's nightmare at Umbara during the Sigil Compact War: fifteen teammates ordered by Cluster Creed to hold ground zero and destroy prototype blueprints as an expendable suicide squad. Choking inside creeping crimson Velocirax-Nyxvirus mist that burned through protective seals, Libra watched brothers and teammates mutate into weeping, apologetic horrors begging for mercy while forced to slaughter their own friends—culminating in a "healthy" rescued teammate leaping onto Libra's little brother and detonating himself!

Barricaded inside a fourth-floor apartment listening to gunfire and shrieks below, survivors froze at Sunset as every single infected corpse stood utterly still staring into the dying sun—before pitch-black blackout nightfall doubled their strength! Hunting via thermal vision through window cracks, acute hearing tuned even to heartbeats, and climbing balcony to balcony as rotting fingernails snapped off, the horde surged behind towering two-meter crimson-eyed Alpha Variants clad in external skeletal bone armor, bloodied steel claws, ribcages split wide like butterfly wings, and dripping acid spit—punching straight through metal doors and tearing Libra's friends apart like Lego bricks. Fleeing past blood-soaked streets, hibernating mall-basement sludge nests holding live human hosts, and a terrifying five-meter conjoined hospital abomination ripping faces off, Libra was shoved aboard an orbital rocket by his captain moments before The Commonwealth glassed the planet with a Planetary Laser Destroyer.

Next stepped forward the frail old seer—who silenced skeptical murmurs instantly by lifting one hand to turn the entire sky purple with halted meteors—introducing himself as Franco, legendary astronomer and Sentinel first responder from 1,022 years ago.

"If you wonder where all the superhumans went—those who lifted mountains and flew at lightspeed—SumireV killed them," Franco revealed grimly. "During her Purging Era, SumireV hunted every civilization harboring reality warpers, gravity users, and superhumans so no rival empire could ever rise against her. When old Intellectual Chronicle clashed with Cluster Creed's millions of superhumans, she dragged massive Rigel toward our planet, personally hunted down every faction's heroes—forcing the universe to pivot toward technology—and confronted my Sentinel commander father with one chilling line: 'Have you ever seen stars shattered?' Casting a point-blank Zeroth Quasi Black Hole, she turned my father into crimson mist before ripping out my eyes, lungs, and lower body."

Asked quietly by a listener to look into the future, Franco meditated silently for several minutes before speaking with heavy sorrow:

"A great war approaches—far worse than anything we've survived. Multiversal empires will invade Universe-26 repeatedly. Cluster Creed, every surviving faction, a reborn Sigil Compact under Orion-TW3, and even the Celestial Council will unite behind SumireV's Core of Absolute. Yet strained beyond breaking, SumireV will die in that war—leaving Universe-26 shattered and collapsing completely behind a sealed lockdown, killing everyone except the Celestials."

Looking silently up at the open sky, Drago whispered a grim hope that death would take him before that day arrived.

Chapter 56 - Independent

Stepping back out of the Realm of Absolute into Universe-4510, SumireV came face-to-face with an Alvitr Zeta elite squad led by Captain Cobalt, who hurled a blazing sun straight at her on sight. Swallowing the star effortlessly inside a red-eyed Quasi Black Hole, SumireV explained calmly that she was the Protector of Humanity from Universe-26 stranded by the Celestial Council—only for Cobalt's squad to open fire anyway out of suspicion. Knocking every rocket, elemental blast, and beam aside with a Crown-style gravitational shockwave, SumireV pounded them with 50 manifested IC battleships and six nuclear warheads before vanishing at lightspeed to construct a quiet hidden bunker on an empty world while waiting on Ethan and Eric.

Days later, inspecting an unfamiliar warship on open radar, SumireV dodged a lightspeed ambush, shattered their Time Lock with Clock-Lock, and manifested TON 618—prompting an immediate ceasefire shout from Vinci and fourteen members of Entropy of Filament, guardians who gathered off-world strays. Shrugging off Vinci's sneaky follow-up lightning test untouched inside her Infinity Barrier, SumireV shook hands and relocated to their outpost after her black hole wrecked the empty planet.

Inside the Entropy base, hostile stares followed the "mere human" until arrogant dark-aura overseer Romulus cornered her at a bench, boasting of his war record and unleashing melting Dark Matter and supernova fists against her unyielding Infinity Barrier.

"Let me flex my power just a bit," SumireV smirked calmly. "Creation Base: 7th Principal Ingenuity—Fallacy of Gods!"

As the colossal crimson Cross manifested alongside holo-footage of her slaying Celestials, Romulus recoiled in utter terror ("IMPOSSIBLE! That Key was used during the Multiversal Great War!") and yielded immediately. Letting her glow fade behind her glasses, SumireV quietly reflected that she had only manifested a copy while still forging her original 10th Solemnity—Tesseract of Human—holding onto quiet conviction: "I've suffered malicious schemes and bitter betrayals, yet witnessed miracles achievable only through kindness and trust. I may seem naive, yet humanity deserves second chances."

Chapter 57 - Reflecting of Past

Stopped by Entropy of Filament Elites after her confrontation with Romulus, SumireV retreated into the nearby barracks to rest. Sitting on her bunk reading a few books she found, she felt wary eyes watching her with quiet disdain, though she brushed it off—reminding herself she was only staying temporarily and wouldn't strike unless provoked. Yet no matter how hard she tried focusing on the pages, memories from centuries past kept pulling her mind away.

Her thoughts drifted back 850 years to an isolated deep-space world in Universe-26 where she had studied the Core of Absolute's 3rd Level—Singularity Rebuild. Her father had once warned her about its infinite density tearing apart space and time; Core wielders normally unleashed it inside pocket dimensions or enemy domains, yet its shockwaves shattered 10 to 15 surrounding dimensions at once, earning it the name Dimension Destruction.

Fast-forwarding through memory to a clash against a Celestial years later, SumireV heard her own voice echoing fiercely across a Celestial Pocket Dimension under rains of Celestial Arrows: "I won't let you destroy my race! Even against divinity, humanity will stand as long as I live!" Shielding behind her Infinity Barrier until her eyes blazed crimson, she had turned the Celestial's own spatial rules back on him: "Core of Absolute, 3rd Level: Singularity Rebuild!" Reality fractured apart cleanly around the disintegrating Celestial, ripping straight into neighboring dimensions.

Suddenly, louder conflicting voices inside her own mind closed in around her—hundreds of years of guilt and fury crashing together at once.

"Sumi, you did well protecting the human race, just like your predecessors," whispered Hero SumireV.

"Why keep protecting humanity when they always betray you?!" snapped Anger SumireV. "Whenever ego takes over, they twist lies, guilt-trip you, and plot behind your back just to feel in control!"

"We aren't nice, perfect, or even good—we are who we are," sighed Kind SumireV.

"You should have slaughtered every bast*rd in your way!" raged Rage SumireV. "Remember your own rule—treat cancer early before it spreads! Why not drop black holes across every faction opposing you?!"

"Humanity is imperfect and ignorant," murmured Wise SumireV gently, "yet not everyone stays that way. Even slim odds deserve a second chance—just like you got while dying in the Liminal War. You taught them reason, logic, and common sense—it's never too late."

"Don't dare pretend everything you did was justified," hissed Vengeful SumireV coldly. "You murdered countless people—bunker survivors blown apart by nuclear bombs, Nano-Virus victims rotting alive, children, the elderly, the sick, the disabled. Think about TON 618 and Sagittarius A*—can you really call yourself a Hero, or just Destruction?"

Overwhelmed by the crushing bottleneck of 1,200 years of memories, SumireV jolted upright when a small child tugged at her sleeve.

"Sister... I heard you groaning and talking in your sleep—it made me worried!"

Before SumireV could answer, the child's mother snatched him back sharply: "Hey! Don't talk to strangers—especially someone from another universe! Who knows what diseases she brought!"

Standing quietly and walking to the barracks window, SumireV watched giant mecha dinosaurs and dragons marching past below alongside dozens of warships. Asking nearby guards, she learned their Governor was launching Operation Thunderstruck against United Grerthaetar. Shaking her head at the reckless campaign, SumireV stayed firmly uninvolved—focused entirely on keeping peace until Ethan and Eric finished her machine.

Chapter 58 - Prototype Test

At last, Ethan and Eric completed the device inside the Realm of Absolute.

"We should call SumireV back now," Eric said.

"Wait—let's run a test first," Ethan suggested.

"Test how?" Eric scoffed. "We can't run it inside the Realm of Absolute—there's no way out from here. It only works in reality. Call SumireV."

Whispering across Core Vision, Ethan brought SumireV hurrying into his laboratory hours later.

"How is the machine?!" she asked immediately.

"Working—at least in theory," Ethan grinned, gesturing alongside Eric toward a sleek console labeled Universal Modular Travel (UMT), capable of crossing three to six universes every few hours. Stepping outside into open space to demonstrate a manifested copy, Ethan walked her through the controls carefully:

"Basically, it works like a jet engine—except instead of breathing air, the reactor inhales Natural Energy. Critical rule: NEVER try halting mid-traversal during a jump, or you'll trigger a cooldown glitch and damage the drive. Push this throttle stick forward to accelerate—my Prometheus tech paired with Eric's Quantum Fission compresses spacetime down to mere kilometers."

"And your upgraded spacesuit will finish refining in a few hours," Eric added. "Study it once it's done and copy it with Creation."

"Ethan," SumireV asked curiously, "can you show me how you mastered Gravity?"

Ethan paused briefly. "You sure? Honestly, exposure against gravity users plus an instinct-enhancement serum—though I won't teach you that serum yet since your cognition baseline is still a bit low." Sighing softly, he raised a hand: "Core of Absolute, Creation Base: Humanity Legion!" Behind him materialized a terrifying armada laced with dark crimson-black gravity energy.

"WHOA—you can summon armadas like I do?!"

"What did you expect from a Core of Absolute user?" Ethan smirked. "Don't be fooled—we specialize in our favorites like Gravity for me or Dimension for Eric, yet every one of us understands the fundamentals across Space, Time, Gravity, Thermodynamics, Acoustics, Quantum, Dimension, Creation, and Electromagnetism. Right now you lean heavily on Dimension, Gravity, Creation, and a touch of Energy from Julian. Watch Eric—Core of Absolute, Dimensional Base: Dimension Illusion!"

Space around SumireV shimmered strangely as Dimension of Logic began bleeding straight into open air. "Wait—how did you do that outside your Dimension?" she gasped.

"You only think it requires stepping inside because you're still new," Eric explained. "With deep mastery and extra energy, you can leak parts of Dimension of Logic directly into reality—even before Level 11, depending on your energy and grasp."

Curious to test Eric's reflexes, SumireV flicked her wrist—launching two thermobaric shells straight at him point-blank! Eric's eyes flashed bright white: "Core Absolute, Creation Base: Mausoleum of Deity!" Instantly sealing SumireV inside a light cube, four energy drainers stabbed down to sap her reserves dry—before SumireV calmly strolled straight back through the harmless simulation. "Whoa, that's insane—in reality that would've killed me!"

"Sealed, not killed," Eric corrected cleanly.

Curious about Solemnities, SumireV asked if they carried Principal Ingenuities. Both men laughed, explaining Kaseng (the 114th user) had destroyed most originals and sealed the rest before dying of Core rejection, preserving every blueprint in memory for successors—leaving SumireV struggling slightly to manifest the 9th Solemnity while Ethan casually flicked between the 5th and 9th, teasing her Zeroth-Level performance against The One before Eric confessed preferring the 2nd Principal Ingenuity (Limit Breaks) and joking SumireV and Ethan ought to get married over their shared gravity obsession!

Growing serious, SumireV asked quietly what Humanity meant to them.

Ethan stared out across the Realm. "Humanity is both flawed and magnificent—emotional, curious, stubborn. Love, hate, envy, peace, experimentation, failure, practice until mastery, error, try again, repeat. That's humanity. As the 262nd wielder, I stood alone between mortals and immortals fighting armies of Deities and Celestials—until unleashing the 7th Solemnity, Fallacy of Gods, and watching fear finally touch Celestial eyes before Core rejection forced me to become a black hole sealing Universe-26."

Resting a fist over his chest, Eric spoke solemnly beside him: "I am Eric, the 234th wielder. I fell defending Universe-44 from Higher Authority invasions, burning across Universe-45 with the 6th Principal Ingenuity—Inferno Unleash, 5th Level: Flame of Reborn—wielding a three-meter greatsword that scorched entire filaments to ash at quintillion-degree heat while burning my own flesh down to bare charred bone! Until my final sight of my daughter's face faded, I refused to let divine harvesters break humanity!"

Overhead, 300 glowing stars aligned beside a bright 301st star as every previous wielder gathered around—Julian, Ragna, Zimmerman, Brian, and the rest—stepping aside as Antonietti Ernst, the 1st wielder, came forward.

"SumireV, our successor—we place our hope in you," Ernst spoke gently. "Reach my Level 20, and you will unlock the Core's second awakening: Core of Absolute Truth. To reach Absolute Truth, you must break past the very barriers helping you now—Reason, Logic, and Common Sense. Accept who you are, reach out to every soul you slew, help them understand why that sacrifice happened, and earn their forgiveness to purify yourself. Where the Core of Absolute shapes endless possibilities, Absolute Truth is a blank sheet of paper—yours to paint a masterpiece, fold origami, or shred into catastrophe."

Placing his palm over her chest, Ernst flooded her mind with the final memories of all 300 protectors before reality pulled her smoothly back—where SumireV immediately reviewed the UMT schematics, manifested the drive via Creation, and ignited her journey home.

Chapter 59 - Transversal

Zipping into her spacesuit, SumireV mounted the UMT and pushed the throttle forward. Spacetime warped and compressed rapidly into narrow corridors as she shot faster than lightspeed past shattered dimensions and defended realms trembling inside the UMT's wake.

Cruising through ancient Corridors of Cosmic—inter-universal highways locked shut by Celestials and higher beings, spotting massive spatial scars carved eons ago by Oslo (the 30th user)—SumireV watched her drive gauge dip low and dropped out of glide into Universe-4492 to recharge.

Drifting cautiously through dark space while pulling up an old holographic map listing Higher Authority domains and sprawling multiverse empires, SumireV monitored millions of distant radio frequencies—freezing briefly as space tore open nearby and an alien armada descended to raid a neighboring solar system. Too tiny to draw attention across deep space with her drive sitting at 30%, SumireV slipped several thousand meters clear and manifested 50 IC Energy Beacon Converters via Creation. Over several quiet hours watching distant Nexus clashes flicker across the void, the drive filled to 100%—and SumireV pushed the UMT to max throttle straight toward hostile space.

Chapter 60 - Intensity Increase

Dropping out of glide inside Universe-4487, SumireV coasted forward until her momentum slowed beneath shattered planets and erratic, dying stars. Sensing hidden ambushers waiting in the dark, she called out sharply: "I know you're hiding, waiting to catch me off guard!"

Triggering an instant IC EMP blast, she stunned a cloaked Trinity Halifax Empire (THE) armada right mid-charge on gamma cannons—immediately following up with thousands of High-Explosive thermobaric shells! Her eyes flaring bright white, SumireV unleashed Creation:

"Core Absolute, Creation Base: Manifest Justification of Authoritarianism Atrocity!"

Blinding orange flashes revealed an armada armed with thermobarics, acids, radiation, nerve agents, biochemicals, and WMDs backed by six Global Abominations raining Tungsten Velocirax-Nyxvirus cluster rods over THE's fleet—drowning crews in crimson mist, peeling flesh, Acute Radiation Syndrome, Pulmonary Fibrosis, and 3rd-degree burns.

"Everyone always thinks I'm just a gentle nun, a soft-spoken protector," SumireV murmured coldly, "forgetting I can destroy anytime, anywhere I choose. Your fate is in my hands."

Suddenly, a devastating strike slammed straight into her back! Regenerating through searing burns, SumireV spun to face an Entropy and several Sentinels scrambled from THE Headquarters. Shrugging off reality warping behind Level 1 Neutronium Stars and manifesting two Divine Bullets, SumireV flared crimson eyes—"Gravity Base: Black Hole Cluster!"—flooding space with swirling black holes to isolate Sentinels while only the Entropy (wielding Crumble, turning touched matter into dust) closed in.

Combining blazing red and white eyes, SumireV strapped a massive crimson-aura Cross onto her back—rapidly draining the Entropy's nearby energy—and charged armed with a gravity-pulling Scythe and Mirror! Matching every scythe swing with twin manifested 155mm Velocirax-Nyxvirus artillery rounds and rifle bursts to overload his senses, SumireV bounced a Planetary Storm straight back off her Mirror and erased a summoned Antares Star inside a Quasi Black Hole—smiling coldly as the cornered Entropy dragged her straight inside his Entropy Domain!

"Hah—I knew he'd use it. Huge mistake: this is a Domain, not a Dimension! Have you ever seen a universe shattered before you?"

"Core Absolute, 3rd Level: Singularity Rebuild!"

Crimson fracture lines crushed the Entropy Domain completely inward around its screaming creator. Stepping cleanly back into real space past crimson mist, SumireV topped her UMT back up from 70% with manifested Energy Beacons and vanished before THE's main reinforcements arrived.

Chapter 61 - Overpressure Trap

Weaving deeper through the hostile multiverse empire controlled by THE—watching entire armadas mobilize relentlessly to hunt her down—SumireV suddenly felt ambient Natural Energy vanish, stalling her UMT mid-flight and dropping her straight into Universe-4450 completely surrounded by thousands of Entropies, Celestials, and endless legions! Insufficient energy left her unable to re-manifest the UMT.

Weakened and outnumbered, SumireV whispered quietly into the dark: "In the end... I'm always alone. Just human—a nun, a woman—dying alone with regrets."

Then a fierce voice burned inside her chest: "SumireV—hero, evil, protector. Why hold back? Why force kindness here? This isn't Universe-26—this is Universe-4450! Drop the excuses, wake up, and show them the might of the human race! Fear is instinct, courage is humanity!"

Her eyes snapped open as hyperfast cognition slowed the swarming armada to a crawl. Unleashing a crushing gravitational push that staggered even the Celestials, SumireV dropped every ounce of restraint.

"You all look like goddamn cockroaches," she snarled coldly. "Think you can take me down? Fine—you'll be my warm-up BEFORE I KILL ALL OF YOU BI*CHES! I only pretended Celestials were intimidating because I held back inside Universe-26—holding back is invalid here! No one's coming to save you—today is your extinction! I am the madness, I am the sickness! Witness: Core Absolute, Maximum Creation Ability—Human Justification of Destruction and Immorality!"

Drawing straight from Core Reserved Energy since Natural Energy sat suppressed, SumireV flooded space with every weapon in memory—armadas, artificial storms, mechs, artillery, nerve agents, bioweapons, incendiaries, and anti-air cannons shredding hordes into crimson mist with hypersonic titanium nets—leaping onto Celestials to rip ribcages and crush hearts while hunting whichever Celestial held the energy suppressant!

Pinned briefly as Celestials bound her arms for reality erasure, SumireV merely smiled through half-shattered flesh and one dangling eye: "Big mistake—your energy suppressant is already dead, BI*CH!" Instantly regenerating as Natural Energy surged back, she raised her arms:

"Creation Base: 7th Solemnity—Fallacy of God!"
