Arriving beneath skies crisscrossed by lasers, Joshua watched Codling safely positioned in the rear beside two Behemoths, manifesting fleets of USS Iowa battleships firing Anti-Cosmic High-Explosive Velocity Shells (ACHEV) that tore deep craters into the Abomination's hide—while Prometheus hovered overhead stacking gravity multiplying tenfold every second (carefully withholding black holes so he wouldn't pull allies in), only for swarms of Overseers to heal the monster's wounds almost instantly.

Meeting briefly with Codling and Prometheus, they locked in a three-way strike over five minutes. Returning to the front beside the Behemoths, Codling unleashed Creation:

"Core of Diversity Potential—give them the Mighty Torrent of Fire and Iron!"

Instantly, every hillside bristled with HIMARS launchers, rockets, missiles, jets, orbital cannons, tanks, and railguns opening fire simultaneously across the Abomination and its Disputed escorts while Codling directed from deep cover out of Destructor range. As enemy elites rushed forward to shield the monster's front, gravity abruptly spiked tenfold per second overhead—shattering structures around them and pinning the Abomination's continent-sized legs deep into the earth!

Dropping directly onto the battlefield below, Joshua pushed his Gem of Eternal Peace to its absolute limit—eyes flaring radiant gold as blinding holy light swallowed his frame:

"Eternal Peace! One cast makes heaven tremble—shiver down your spine! ETERNAL PEACE: RESTRAIN FROM HEAVEN!"

Slamming his palm onto the earth, glowing fissures split across the continent straight beneath the Abomination—sinking its colossal legs entirely underground before six towering, thorny light stakes erupted through its hide, hung with hundreds of miniature 7th Principal Ingenuity Replicas draining 40% of local enemy energy! Raining millions of holy daggers, spears, and swords over scrambling Overseer healers, Joshua snapped a colossal chain of light around the monster's throat to pin its skull motionless:

"Codling—your turn! END IT!"

Manifesting an Orbital Annihilation cannon, Codling fired point-blank into the beast's face—only for its thick hide to resist the beam! Immediately, Prometheus hailed Devin to open an energy-link portal uniting all three fighters. Supercharged by the link, Joshua's chains clamped iron-tight, Prometheus anchored space with a roaring White Hole, and Codling bombarded the monster's face with Velocirax-Nyxvirus shells to strip its physical defenses before firing a localized Radical Collapse (tuned to 30% Solar-System output)—blowing the Abomination's skull into crimson spray across the continent!

Miles away, Vihaan and Royal Guard troops heard the thunderous victory just as massive allied paratrooper drops flooded southern Cluster Creed: 19 trillion Orion troops, 600,000 IC specialists, 11 trillion Tectonic Pact fighters, and 4 trillion Bureau Coalition troops—while The Empire landed 200 trillion soldiers in the Northeast near the Marrow Energy route, severing Arcadian supply lines to Order of North (giving Polar Starborne vital relief) even while facing 601 septillion elite Arcadians boxed in between Serenity Cliff (NW), The Empire (NE), and the GAU (S).

Chapter 78 - Another Front for Arcadian?

Deep inside the Arcadian Hive Front, GAU Cosmic Division Marshall Zou stepped forward to meet Commander Siemen of Centauri Enigma (flagship power of the Ascendancy Brigration Alliance).

"I'm from the GAU," Zou introduced himself evenly. "Who are you, and what brings you here?"

"I'm from Centauri Enigma," Siemen replied briskly. "We're here to stop the Arcadians from pouring endless legions into our Ascendancy Brigration Alliance—cutting them off at the root! Even while their spores still choke our homeland, our Emperor ordered us to strike the Hive directly."

Zou smiled smoothly. "In that case, how about teaming up against them? We'll strike from the East backed by our finest Artificial Celestials."

"Agreed—we know the Top 1 Military's reputation," Siemen nodded before leaning closer at Zou's question regarding other unfamiliar corpses scattered across the ruins. "There are over seven factions and two full alliances already bleeding inside this Hive—Celebus Entente, Ascendancy Brigration, and an insanely advanced rocket-tech faction called Imperium Orosian Hegemony (IOH). We used to be rivals until the Arcadians wiped out trillions—even quintillions—of us. IOH barely fields cosmic troops, yet their missile tech feels out of this universe; they were the very first to storm the Hive, pouring troops like water into a bucket and burning 90 quintillion dead in a single month out of pure rage and grief!"

Handing Zou a tactical map showing Celebus Entente focusing on orbital LZ suppression before pushing inland, Siemen added quietly: "Honestly, the only faction that truly knows how to beat Arcadians is Cluster Creed—an ancient Cluster Creed hero sealed them millennia ago, though from what we heard, Cluster Creed is occupied right now." Together, GAU and Centauri Enigma began mapping joint assaults on the Hive.

Back inside Cluster Creed, GAU and Empire columns crossed paths tensely without firing—until GAU Commander Adam unexpectedly broke radio silence across shared channels to address ZQC, Cluster Creed, and The Empire directly:

"This is Commander Adam of the GAU. Every force inside Cluster Creed needs to know other external alliances have been attacking the Arcadian Hives long before we arrived—taking catastrophic losses while bleeding the Arcadians across multiple fronts. I know Attrition Titan despises us, yet for now let's hold fire against one another whenever Arcadians are present. Once they're dead, we can go back to killing each other."

Stunned to learn unknown factions existed outside their sphere, Codling immediately dispatched six fast stealth scout fleets straight toward the Arcadian Hive to verify the frontlines.

Meanwhile, back on Serenity Cliff, learning his first wave had been cut in half, Franco walked into his armory and climbed back into his old Telekinesis battlesuit—equipping three Crystals alongside his father's Gem of Mischief. Stepping outside onto a cliffside to test his strength, Franco clenched one fist—imploding an entire mountain to dust in silence!

"Well... haven't used this since my first fight against SumireV," Franco smiled faintly through mechanical eyes, boarding a mothership leading the second wave straight toward mainland Cluster Creed.

Chapter 79 - The Attrition Master

Deep inside the Pivot Federation's Red Zone, Crown hovered on comms with Isabella.

"My friends inside The Empire say they're dealing with way stronger Arcadian elites there because The Empire deployed four Core users—whereas here we're mostly facing regular Arcadian armies," Crown reported.

"Good—that makes massive AoE wipes super easy," Isabella replied briskly. "I'll solo the Northeast Red Zone against 500 quadrillion Arcadians and any elites that drop. Since you're young—and your squad is here—take the Northwest Red Zone's 730 quadrillion alongside Zero, Klein, Mao, the PF Army, and an inbound Behemoth."

Rocketting into hypersonic flight beside Zero (newly upgraded by ZQC researchers for far larger nuclear yields), Crown laid out the plan cleanly over team comms:

"Klein and PF forces will fortify deep inside the mountain ridge while Klein taunts the Arcadians straight toward the center so they won't flank us. Mao channels massive Natural Energy straight into Zero, Klein, and me so nobody runs dry. Zero and I hit from high altitude with max airburst AoE—then PF heavy artillery and Klein's synthetic tanks mop up whatever survives. Clear?"

"Clear!" all three answered cleanly.

Digging deep into the fortified ridge alongside Pivot Federation troops armed with new Splitter Laser railguns (firing 3 to 6 lethal beams per shot), Klein erected massive speakers across the peaks while Mao linked shimmering Natural Energy lines directly to her, Zero, and Crown.

"You 'Deity' race think you're better than humans?!" Klein's voice boomed furiously across the valley. "Not even close! Faces like shrimp and bodies like cockroaches—you're just genetically degraded defects! Angry because SumireV killed Aetheris?! Stay mad—come find me on this mountain and prove you aren't total disappointments!"

Screaming in blind rage, hundreds of millions of Arcadians abandoned every flank and surged straight toward the mountain center!

"Enjoy!" Crown grinned as Mao flooded energy straight into them—Crown and Zero plunging from orbit like twin meteors! Zero detonated a colossal nuclear blast wiping half the horde while Crown shattered the remaining half! PF batteries and Klein's synthetic tanks hammered incoming Arcadian dropships while Zero soared overhead spitting continuous gamma beams that melted twenty warships a second. Whenever Crown tried diving into melee, Klein yanked his leg back with synthetic tethers ("Stick to long range—create piles of HE shells right in front of me!"), letting Crown launch thousands of high-explosive shells at hypersonic speed with Frontal Impulse Force as an inbound Dreadnought arrived to hammer the Arcadian Mothership!

Two hours later, Mao faltered under heavy energy drain just as an even larger Arcadian armada poured down from orbit—overwhelming Klein's trenches and forcing Mao onto defense. Charging straight forward behind a force bubble firing his Anti-Living Handcannon at Temporals and Overseers, Crown fought beside Zero—who unleashed his final stage of Radical Ionizing, melting anything within twenty meters on contact while raining glowing liquid-uranium Zeta Rays from the sky! Still, the horde pressed relentlessly inward.

Out of options, Crown and Zero caught each other's eyes in the storm, clasped hands, and began to overcharge.

"I don't care if I die," Zero smiled calmly, "as long as humanity lives—that's enough to put me at peace."

"OI, KLEIN—GET EVERYONE OUT NOW! WE CAN'T STOP THEM!" Mao screamed, dragging thousands of PF troops clear on energy currents while Klein manifested emergency transports straight into a sudden escape portal opened by Devin. Barely diving through at the final instant, Klein watched from safety as a blinding emerald sphere of Impulse Force and Radiation swelled across twenty entire planets, compressed violently inward, and detonated:

"Zeroth Critical Mass Force Annihilation!!"

Blinding green light engulfed space—obliterating over five entire sectors, rattling Isabella miles away, and throwing Mao unconscious onto a drifting planetary chunk.

Waking amid floating debris and lethal radiation clouds, Mao tracked Klein's beacon—both looking up in astonishment as Codling stepped straight out of Devin's portal: "WHAT THE FU*K JUST HAPPENED?! We literally felt the wind from that shockwave back in Cluster Creed!"

Before they could explain, a battered Isabella teleported down carrying barely breathing Crown and Zero! "Here are your teammates—patching them up cost 80% of my Core Reserve, though they'll survive," Isabella panted, smiling wryly. "Not gonna lie—you ZQC fighters are unbelievably strong even without Cores. There's literally zero Arcadians left in Pivot Federation now—and our government didn't even blink at troop deaths, just thrilled the Arcadians are gone. Gotta rest—see y'all soon!"

Quickly kissing Codling on the cheek before teleporting away, Isabella left a blinking Codling to order Devin to rush Crown and Zero straight into Bureau Coalition medical bays while Mao and Klein headed back to assist surviving PF wounded.

Chapter 80 - Arcadian Solution Required

Inside The Commonwealth, delegates from across Universe-26 gathered for an emergency Galaxy Group Meeting. The Government Authority representative paced anxiously waiting for everyone to arrive, tension hanging thick across the chamber as 26 alliances and 91 factions packed the tiered seats—Zenith Quadrant Citadel arriving last, with Codling taking his seat feeling slightly nervous beneath so many watchful stares.

The Commonwealth representative rose first. "I hereby call all available factions together to discuss the Arcadian threat ravaging sectors of Universe-26. The Commonwealth urges every faction in this room to cooperate and share intelligence openly about the Arcadians—not to seize advantage over rivals, but for the survival of us all. Thank you."

Order of North's representative stood immediately. "We are Order of North, and we've fought Arcadian Lord Class commanders since the very first day they appeared. According to our field reports, Lord Class Arcadians drive Hive expansion and spawning as regional leaders—identifiable wherever Arcadian death spores grow visibly denser."

Centauri Enigma's delegate spoke next. "As leader of the Ascendancy Brigration Alliance, we declared war on the Arcadians two months after the Cluster Creed outbreak, when they launched a conquest foothold into our territory. We pushed them out and struck straight at their Hive."

Imperium Orosian Hegemony's representative stepped forward grimly. "We want to share what we found inside the Arcadian Hive. Being the first faction to land there, we uncovered ruined planets and rescued traumatized survivors choked by Arcadian spores. One survivor recovered enough to tell us everything: three entire alliances and over 26 factions once existed inside that sector! Every single one fell to the Arcadians' strongest units, their populations harvested into Arcadian soldiers! We urge attacking the Hive directly to end this threat and liberate whoever remains."

Rising swiftly, the GAU representative addressed the hall. "Listen, all of us—I know we hold bitter grudges against one another, yet we have to unite first to defeat the Arcadians as our common enemy! We GAU already poured four quadrillion troops into Cluster Creed amid relentless Arcadian ambushes, and recently mobilized five quintillion conventional soldiers from every corner of GAU space just to free Universe-26! Every faction—no matter how neutral—must fight to put an end to this! And we need Radiant Guard to join in and back Orosian's assault from the South!"

Codling stood up calmly. "I'm fairly new to this era and didn't know the Arcadians' history at all, though our ZQC tech wiped them out cleanly inside our territory. Cluster Creed—would you mind sharing the full history, and how your ancient hero sealed them away?"

Cluster Creed's delegate sighed heavily. "The Arcadians are a formidable force. For two thousand years they ravaged Universe-26, wiping out dozens of factions. Worse, the Celestial Council can't even touch them—during that era, Arcadians captured top-tier ancient tech granting total immunity against Celestial attacks. Cluster Creed stood as the sixth-to-last surviving faction fighting them for decades until launching one desperate gamble: not lethal force, but a colossal illusion mimicking Demi-God strikes—blinding energy and massive explosions with near-zero real damage—tricking the Arcadians into retreating in terror until we cornered and sealed them. Over millennia, the seal deteriorated after our hero passed away, bringing them back upon this era. We didn't have a permanent way to kill them all—only sealing them until future generations built weapons strong enough to finish the job."

Imperial Phalanx's leader stepped up to call the vote. Ninety-two percent of the chamber pressed Agree—Radiant Guard notably abstaining for the moment ("We aren't prepared yet; give us time, though scouts already spotted Arcadians along our horizon, so we will join eventually").

Wrapping up the summit, Orion-TW3 proposed a leaner Universe-26 war group limited to four representatives per faction back at Commonwealth to avoid overcrowding. Flying back at lightspeed as Commonwealth's war reactors hummed visibly across deep space, Codling touched down at Interstellar Chronicle and immediately opened ZQC comms:

"This is Codling speaking. ZQC will cooperate under this temporary coalition—finishing Cluster Creed's liberation first before pushing into eastern Attrition Titan space, attacking Arcadians from the West out of Galaxy Sector K16. Stay sharp mentally and physically—we're about to make history."

Across Attrition Titan space, Imperial Phalanx, The Empire, and Pivot Federation finally caught their breath as homefronts cleared—rebuilding rapidly to reinforce Cluster Creed. Imperial Phalanx deployed first via ZQC's southern corridor; The Empire lagged slightly hunting Marrow Energy; and Pivot Federation worked tirelessly reconstructing its shattered Deep Red Zone—over 90% destroyed at a cost of 20 septillion lives from Crown and Zero's final detonation—as every eye in the universe turned toward the Arcadian Hive.

Chapter 81 - Infiltration of the Strongest Alliance

Slipping silently across the southern border of Zenith Quadrant Citadel's occupied Hunt territory, two Government Authority stealth ships crept toward the Zenith Quadrant Citadel Combined Researcher Project (ZQCCRP)—dispatched personally by Admiral Blackbear to steal IC's newest classified blueprints.

Aboard the lead craft, twenty elite GAU agents prepped for breach, spearheaded by Joseph's six-man point squad: Razor (whose ability let his blade slice any physical alloy like paper and seal the cut cleanly behind them, though unable to tear open dimensions), Faizan (the team scanner), Bello (data thief), and Grail (flame user).

"Cut it, Razor," Joseph whispered outside the facility wall. Razor nodded, slicing neatly through hardened plating while the squad slipped inside and sealed the seam behind them.

Reaching Room 29, Grail glanced nervously down the corridor. "Think we might walk into an ambush?"

"Relax, son—ZQC is tied down in Attrition Titan out West, so even their leader isn't here," Joseph reassured briskly. "Besides, this is a Research HQ, not a military base—defenses will be light."

Yet after sweeping room after room with Faizan scanning and Bello rifling files, they found zero vaults and nothing classified—only mundane digital logs, hobby notes, and outdated news confirming IC and Orion had deployed into The Empire. Resigned, Joseph hailed Admiral Blackbear to admit failure—listening grimly as Blackbear raged furiously over comms before turning back to his crew.

"Nothing we can do—I'll take the blame and probably a demotion. Let's get back to the hangar and pull out."

Reaching the hangar bay, they froze in horror: the rest of their twenty-man team—including their Herald unit—hung dead across the bay, skewered onto sharp wooden spikes. Stepping out of the shadows stood Dakari.

"Kill him—don't give him an opening!" Joseph yelled.

Grail instantly loosed 1,000-degree flames to burn away Dakari's energy-draining Pollinated Bloom while Razor sliced through erupting vines—until Dakari shouted: "Forest Mode: Deep Amazon Rainforest Emerging!" Thick jungle exploded across the entire hangar, severing comms beneath choking yellow spores that starved GAU battle suits dry. Splitting apart inside the hostile canopy, Razor fought frantically toward his teammates until stumbling across Faizan's corpse hanging mutilated among thorns and three wooden spears.

A massive tree-bark fist slammed Razor crashing across the dirt before Joseph and Grail dropped beside him—Joseph hammering Dakari at supersonic speed while Grail seared him in swirling flame as Dakari sponged blows with oak arms and twisted Joseph's wrist! Spotting Razor charging his sword for a massive cleave ("RAZOR, GO! GET HIM!"), Dakari evaded cleanly—only to take Grail's full flame blast and Joseph's Cluster Punch straight in the chest.

Instead of falling, Dakari stood tall smiling—and lifted smoothly off the ground into flight!

"Wait—he can fly?!" Razor gasped.

"I'm a Superhuman from Tidal Pulsar—born after SumireV's Purge Era," Dakari smirked coldly. "And I'm a Commander in IC's military. That was just my natural body—now witness Serum Phaser-XI!"

Igniting Variant S (Sole/Solid) at Phase 66%, Dakari crushed Joseph's fist barehanded, hurled him straight through a GAU stealth ship in a hypersonic slugfest, shattered his jaw, and surged to Phase 84%—punching straight through Joseph's chest to yank his cervical spine inward and crush his skull instantly!

"YOU BASTARD!!" Grail shrieked, transforming into a blazing solar avatar ("Flame of Sanctuary: Embodiment of Burns!") that engulfed the entire hangar in incinerating fire—disintegrating Dakari's bark armor and scorching flesh straight off his bones! Yet cranking Serum Phaser Variant S straight to 100%, Dakari walked calmly right through the inferno regenerating step by step until Grail's exhausted flame guttered back into human form—driving an arm straight through Grail's torso before slamming a colossal log down across him, flattening Grail into paste on the floor!

"I surrender—don't kill me!" Razor yelled, dropping his blade instantly.

Resting a hand on Razor's shoulder with a faint smirk ("Wise choice"), Dakari snatched him at hypersonic speed to temporary holding—bound for Homes of Ignorant—before rocketing straight out into deep space.

Meanwhile, far away inside Universe-541, a lone figure stood inside a battered armory of the Republic of Stars Strings—completely untouched inside an Infinity Barrier after Major Rosaria saturated the complex until its walls collapsed.

"I am the mighty host of the human race, and I will stand bearing everything against any foe in my path," SumireV spoke evenly. "Humanity has fought for every moment of its survival. You hold the numbers—yet Fear is Instinct, Courage is Humanity, and Humanity is your downfall."

Instantly, 10 Billion World War II Yamato Battleships materialized across space, unleashing beehive thermobaric salvos while Phoenix A disrupted Natural Energy and warped time across their armada! Gravity Disarray mangled two lightspeed Sentinels on approach; when six Archons locked her Core temporarily, SumireV blurred instantly on Vulcan-E2 lightning tech to slaughter the locker; and as 200 Gem- and Crystal-armed elites converged, SumireV smirked and invoked 9th Principal Ingenuity Singularity Engine, Level 4: Stellar Force—compressing two entire galactic sectors!

Even as Celestials and Deities descended from above, SumireV flared "Core of Absolute, 5th Level: Terror of Human Creation—BEFALL!" alongside 7th Principal Ingenuity—flooding every divine sensor with unbearable infinity agony without granting quick death: "I'm going home, and I'll destroy anything in my path!"

Suddenly, reality itself fractured open above Universe-541 as a True Deity stepped through wielding the 3rd Principal Ingenuity: Creation of Fundamental!

"Still relying on human-forged weapons to fight humans?" SumireV scoffed coldly, manifesting the 9th Principal Ingenuity in her left hand and the 7th across the galactic core. "You use Principal Ingenuities because it's the only weapon you think works—I use them because they're the only weapons known to slaughter False Deities like you!"

"Let's see what you've got, human," the Deity answered evenly.

Chapter 82 - Pivot Federation Attrition Might

Nine days after Crown and Zero's catastrophic detonation across Pivot Federation space, a handful of stubborn Arcadian pockets still held out inside the ruined City of Long Lost in the Deep Red Zone.

Hiding inside battered concrete with 65,000 survivors under an eight-day siege, Major Kruxos stared grimly at his empty supply racks. Their Hive was gone, Captain Ziban's neighboring pocket had fallen silent, and no reinforcements were coming. Ordering a Signal Arcadian up to the rooftop, Kruxos watched him detonate skyward into a tall laser flare—warning any scattered survivors they still stood.

Sharp at midnight, thunderous Pivot Federation artillery tore open the silence.

"Here they come—ENGAGE!" Kruxos roared over comms as tanks, mechanical bears, and heavy Federation infantry rolled down the avenues.

For a frantic stretch, Pivot Federation Silent Penetration rounds traded cleanly against Arcadian Destructor Bullets—both piercing straight through each other's armor—before Arcadian Beasts surged out from side streets, ripping through front ranks and forcing Federation forces back!

Silence fell briefly before a second, heavier artillery storm flattened city blocks, followed by strategic bombers carpeting streets in incendiaries and Dyfra-101 chemical clouds, tanks, APCs, laser turrets, and dense rocket salvos. Mechanical bears leaped straight onto charging Arcadian Beasts to pin them down and self-destruct, while Federation flamethrowers modified to burn at cosmic intensity swept ground floors room by room!

Watching every defensive perimeter buckle, Kruxos barked over comms: "All remaining Arcadian forces—fight until your final breath! Never surrender to those little humans!"

Outside, Pivot Federation loudspeakers blared: "This is the Federation Army! You are surrounded—surrender now or die! This is your last chance!"

Snapping a clean shot straight through the soldier holding the megaphone, Kruxos vanished beneath a blinding hail of return gunfire. Pushing steadily toward the city square, Federation forces crushed wounded Arcadians beneath tank treads, choked troops whose masks cracked in Dyfra-101 fumes, burned basement holdouts alive, and pounded the final 2,000 survivors against 600,000 soldiers under point-blank mortar blasts until the guns finally stopped.

Waking stripped of his armor and missing both legs amid looting crews, one final surviving Arcadian looked up in terror as a Federation Wind user stepped forward ("So this is where I clean up?"). Summoning a screaming tornado that swept up the broken Arcadians, the Wind user spun the vortex faster until pressure ripped the prisoner's back open and sucked his organs straight out into a blood-red cyclone—while Federation Chemical crews sprayed Dyfra-A02 acid mist to dissolve every lingering spore, leaving the Wind user to fly calmly onward toward twenty more corpse-filled districts.

Chapter 83 - Turning Tide

Sixty-one days after the Commonwealth summit, Cluster Creed stood liberated from main Arcadian armies—at staggering cost across every coalition: ZQC lost 200 quadrillion soldiers; Attrition Titan lost 119 septillion; Supreme Administrative Bloc lost 528 quintillion (mostly GAU) alongside 293 quadrillion from Commonwealth; and Polar Starborne lost 50 septillion.

Inside ZQC's occupied Hunt headquarters, Codling studied the intergalactic encirclement closing around the Arcadian Hive:

Northwest: GAU, Commonwealth, and Celebus Entente.

North: Polar Starborne Alliance (Order of North, Sovereign of Eclipse, and Parliament Abstract).

East: Attrition Titan (The Empire and Pivot Federation, leaving Imperial Phalanx and Cluster Creed guarding homefronts).

Southwest: Zenith Quadrant Citadel (Orion-TW3 and Interstellar Chronicle).

South: Imperium Orosian Hegemony and Radiant Guard.

Northeast: Fascist Vega Foundation Alliance (Seraphim Extinction and Ordinal Cardinality).

West: Ascendancy Brigration Alliance anchoring the terrain they knew best.

Briefing Codling on intelligence extracted from Razor, Dakari warned that Polar Starborne and SAB had deployed at least five Core users in SumireV's absence; Klein blushed slightly reporting Pivot Federation had held its Core users back while pouring in a 50-septillion expedition army; and MiRi confirmed The Empire's dictator was marching personally to war. Frowning quietly as he realized reopening the Marrow Energy route allowed rivals to unleash top assets, Codling set Operation Eradication Arcadian for one week out—murmuring silently to himself that it was time to bring back "the Overseer of Interstellar Chronicle."

Three days later, standing in his office watching hangars overflow with MiRi and Dr. Zack's mechs and nanobot containers while Ampasy trained lifting miniature planets outside, Codling turned quietly toward Klein.

"Klein, my dear... this is our final battle against the Arcadian race," he murmured softly. "I'm sorry I'm not perfect like SumireV—I'm just terrified I won't make it back alive, or that I might lose you. Our journey is still so long, and we're still young."

Cupping his face gently, Klein leaned close. "I know, Cody—I'm scared of losing you too. Still, we'll fight our hardest to come back alive. Remember SumireV's rule: play safe, stay alert, stay alive, and watch each other's backs. You're my everything—promise you'll watch my back too."

"I promise, Klein," Codling whispered, pulling her into a tight embrace before ZQC's vanguard shifted smoothly to the Hive's southeastern buffer zone.

Twelve hours before launch on Day 7, Codling stepped before countless assembled ZQC troops, his voice ringing clear across every frequency:

"Today, my brothers and sisters, is the day we've been waiting for—the invasion of the Arcadian Hive! For months we endured their terror, watching families mourn, lives lost, and grief no wealth can ever repay. Yet ZQC stood firm through every moment of survival under one truth: Humanity is weak—mere flesh and blood without innate cosmic power or tech—yet we possess Mind, and Mind births infinite possibilities! Love, hate, joy, sorrow, brilliance, survival—that is humanity! Those Arcadians want us extinct, harvested, violated—do you want to be erased?! NO! We refuse, and we resist!

"Are you ready to fight—and die if we must—for the sake of humanity?! In this war, there is NO second place: first place is victory, and second place is destruction! Let those false deities learn the human race is their doomsday! Fear is instinct, Courage is humanity—COURAGE! RAISE YOUR ARMS!"

Every soldier in the staging sector erupted in a deafening battle cry as Operation Eradication Arcadian launched across the buffer zones—Codling smiling quietly to himself: "A gift is awaiting."

Two days earlier, Codling had walked alone inside an eerie, abandoned IC estate—finding a solitary figure floating in mid-air clutching a glowing Core.

"Who the fu*k are you?" she snapped coldly. "I'm not in the mood to talk about my sister."

"Please—you're the strongest fighter I know, second only to SumireV!" Codling urged.

Dropping cleanly to the floor, she glared—memories flashing past of her past atrocities: dropping a literal sun onto the Setpy Race to exterminate them overnight; obliterating planets simply by tearing past in lightspeed gravity wakes; and drowning unfavored enemy cities in Zegntek, a colorless, odorless gas causing victims breathing open air to feel themselves drowning slowly, horrors that sickened even Joshua and SumireV.

"Buddy, want me to kill you? Fifth visitor this month asking about SUMIREV! I'm NOT a nun like my sister!"

"We're trying to end the Arcadian threat once and for all—and we need YOU, Estella!"

Halting mid-glow, she blinked. "You know my name? Who the heck are you?"

"I'm Codling."

"Oh—didn't see your face in the dark, my bad. You want me on the frontline?"

"To rally ZQC morale—let everyone know even SumireV's sister fights beside them!"

"Isn't your Creation Core enough?"

"I haven't mastered it yet—not like you, a natural Core user."

Stepping forward with a wicked smile, Estella pressed one finger softly against Codling's lips: "Shhh~ Maybe I will join the frontline... haven't tested my powers since Sigil Compact, hehe~" Opening her palm to reveal her blazing Core of Human Envious Desire ("creates anything I deeply want!"), she grinned wildly ("Give me the order when it starts, okay?") before blasting straight upward through the roof at lightspeed—leaving a blinking Codling to manifest an SR-71 Blackbird and fly back to command.

Chapter 84 - The Overseer

Eight hundred years before the Hive campaign, long before the Sigil Compact War, SumireV stood alone against the Sovereign of Eclipse's elite Hercules Rank led by Commander Alem.

"Sending your finest troops just to stop one person?" SumireV called evenly.

"I am Alem, our Emperor's final trump card—and we will end you!" Alem declared, signaling barrages of Celestial Beams—only for SumireV to swallow every ray inside a moon-sized Quasi Black Hole and counter with 200 manifested TOS-1 and Katyusha launchers whose explosives tore straight past their stone barriers!

Manifesting a Scythe on Vulcan-E1 lightning speed, SumireV sliced swiftly toward Alem—falling straight into his trap as a concealed Celestial Beam sheared her lower body clean away! Regenerating rapidly, she barely braced before six mummified Undead Legends—fallen heroes preserved from Sovereign of Eclipse's ancient war against the Arcadians—blitzed her from every blind spot simultaneously, hurling her straight into an active volcano so violently her arms and legs snapped cleanly!

"DON'T GIVE HER A SECOND TO CAST A BLACK HOLE!" Alem barked.

Exhausted after fighting since the previous day with her battle suit failing, SumireV forced Serum Phaser Phase 3—dragging two Undead Legends down before an empty energy gauge and a blazing Solar Shatter Ray hurled her crashing across a mountain peak thousands of kilometers away. Rain poured down into puddles reflecting half her ruined face—bare skull and torn flesh visible, one hand shattered completely and the other stripped to bleeding bone as her Core Integrity plunged to 21%, IC reinforcements still a week away, and dozens of furious Hercules Rank soldiers closing in for the kill.

CRASH!

Out of the clear sky slammed a lightspeed impact like a nuclear warhead! Stepping smoothly out of the crater, Estella sliced Alem's head clean off and crushed his skull underfoot!

"At last you came, older sister..." SumireV breathed weakly. "Sorry I look like this."

"Oh, shut it, Sumi—Ampasy and MiRi are coming to extract you," Estella scoffed gently. "I'll handle these pests."

As the surviving Undead Legends lunged at hypersonic speed, Estella spread her arms—tearing open a Dark Matter rift that yanked the entire battlefield inside another dimension! Answering their Solar Shatter Rays with her Core of Human Envious Desire ("Mimic!"), she fired an even deadlier ray straight from her chest before two Sovereign of Eclipse Entropies clamped down to drain her energy while four Ultra Nuclear Rockets detonated directly over her!

Emerging bleeding yet grinning wildly inside a blinding pillar of purple-yellow light, Estella roared: "I am Estella, the Might and Embodiment of Humanity's Ego!" Sweeping walls of 2-megaton-TNT light and Neutron Star blasts across half the planet, she blurred straight past lightspeed directly into Sovereign of Eclipse's Grand Court—landing like a nuclear bomb amid hundreds of defending Sentinels!

"Can you... stop me?" she whispered, shattering the planet beneath her palm—only for Sovereign of Eclipse's resident Core user to warp reality and restore the world whole!

"Neutronium Stars Cluster Explosion!" Enraged, Estella obliterated 120 planets in seconds, punched straight through the Core user's chest, ripped his Core out to disable him, and hurled him alive inside a dimensional painting to hang trapped forever unless rescued! Pursued across space by the Serpent Division, Estella carved reality open with her blade, summoning a colossal Demi-God hand engulfing half of Sovereign of Eclipse before dropping a single volatile Dark Matter bead that reversed time briefly and tore their megastructures apart!

Six days later back at IC, watching news reports of 80% of Sovereign of Eclipse (over 2,000 worlds) left in ruins—forcing SoE to lean heavily on Commonwealth and Orion-TW3 aid—SumireV furiously seized Estella by the collar:

"Was that even necessary?! First The Empire, then Cluster Creed, then Orion, and now Sovereign of Eclipse—you just painted a giant bullseye onto IC! We have to abandon this sector completely and brace for endless waves!"

"It HAD to be done so they wouldn't think we're weak!" Estella snapped, shoving SumireV back. "Besides, if your people die, that's their problem!" Blasting straight through the ceiling at lightspeed, she left SumireV ordering immediate chemical-warfare expansion and deep fortification—while inside The Commonwealth, shattered representatives from SoE, Cluster Creed, and Orion-TW3 united formally into the Sigil Compact Coalition!

Finishing her story back in the present, Estella glanced quietly at Codling and watched Prometheus step forward to clear the Arcadian Hive landing zone.

Chapter 85 - The Final String of Hope

This was the war that scarred Universe-26 forever—a war so merciless that every survivor grew silent at the mere mention of Sigil Compact: 500 quadrillion cosmic and divine warriors drawn from Cluster Creed, Sovereign of Eclipse, The Commonwealth, and a sliver of Orion-TW3, marching to exterminate a single human-led faction: Interstellar Chronicle.

Two hundred fifty years earlier, SumireV lay locked inside a ten-year coma after slaying the Big Seven Sentinels, Estella lingered wounded inside a restoration chamber, and IC's resources scraped empty. Stepping up to IC's outer ring, Joshua rallied the final 100,000 survivors of IC's Pinnacle Division—missing limbs, scarred, yet unbroken—beside young Han (missing fingers, clutching his futuristic Sword of a Thousand Heroes that copied slain foes' combat memories upon tasting blood) and a Shadow-user volunteering to die buying time.

"We are at the brink!" Joshua cried out. "Remember SumireV's words—past cold mountain shadows lies sunlit warmth! When no path exists, forge one; if a mountain blocks us, go around it or dig a tunnel! Rise and fight the Sigil Compact!"

Grabbing pain suppressants, all 100,000 marched out onto the Ring—Han plunging IC's flag straight into a hilltop ("Rather die than kneel!"), Nuclear Engineering priming GS-1s and Energy Department firing thundering Anti-Matter Cannons—as countless portals ripped open across space pouring 500 quadrillion invaders straight into Harper's kill zones!

Carnage engulfed the sector. Wind users drowned both armies inside lethal blinding hurricanes; a Nova fighter split an enemy Command Ship in half with his own detonating body; Riven carved dozens of warships apart with giant shadow claws; Nuclear Engineering's chief melted himself into a living bomb deleting half an armada; Damien (Top 5) smashed 50 Archons; Atika (Top 9) flung pulverized planets at lightspeed; David died solo holding 50 Sentinels after imploding an SoE command bridge; and The Death March—Harper (Top 3, dual daggers and three Gems), Jacob/The King (Top 12, elemental master), Damien (Top 5, Space Shocker + 7 Crystals), Barry (Top 6, overclocked Time), and Argus (Top 8, blood-draining vampire)—plunged straight into impossible kill zones!

Down to 19,000 fighters in that sector, Top 16 Herman Siegfried—scorched down to a blackened skeleton with only heart and brain beating—staggered upright:

"Flame of Destruction: Total Annihilation Mode! 6th Principal Ingenuity Replica: Inferno Unleash—Level 3: Totalitarianism of Human Cruelty!" Freezing 2.2 billion warships in slow motion before detonating them ablaze, Herman burned out his final sixty seconds in "4th Level: Inferno of Death—Downfall of Divinity!"—vaporizing himself completely to erase the entire advancing wave!

Across the line among 42,000 surviving defenders, Han—having lost one arm completely—jabbed IC's flagpole straight into his severed stump, hugged an overloaded Anti-Matter cell, and fired his autopilot suit under lethal gunfire straight into an enemy mothership, blowing out their portal anchors just as Estella triggered Robust Mode and PROTOCOL-11, sending IC's cosmic vanguard charging the final 500-quadrillion wave!

When the smoke cleared, only 200 out of 100,000 Pinnacle defenders remained alive—Joshua sitting weeping quietly in blood-soaked mud beside fallen Holy Division nuns and priests while Damien hauled IC dead away and burned enemy corpses, Iflaren mourned Bani, Kelly, Marian, Zaman, Shawn, and David, and Harper staggered off with a torn kidney toward restoration chambers—right as Dr. Pedro unleashed second-wave Velocirax-Nyxvirus rockets through portals straight into Sigil Compact homeworlds!

Within thirty days, unstoppable viral mutation shattered the Sigil Compact Coalition completely—ending at a horrific toll of 2 nonillion dead across all sides (including GAU/Empire expeditionaries and neutral suppliers), 70,000 destroyed planets, and a third of shattered veterans later taking their own lives, propelling IC to #3 superpower and #1 in technology while birthing Order of North, Parliament Abstract, and Tectonic Pact from the ashes.

Years later, SumireV listened back to the archived survivor recordings preserved for posterity: Argus describing waking up praying only to kill as many as possible; Damien swearing never to bend to cosmic tyranny; Harper recalling waking up wondering if each shift was his last; Atika throwing shattered mountains at lightspeed between shift bells; and finally the cassette labeled The King (Jacob):

"I used to be scared—sitting in rear lines praying alongside my mama for the dead," Jacob's calm, cold voice echoed from the recording. "Until fear turned to sadness... sadness to anger... anger to hatred... hatred to rage... and rage into cold cruelty! My eyes dried up completely; I stepped onto the frontlines programmed solely to exterminate! They deemed humans weak and tried to crush us—so they forced me to become strong! Now the weak must be purged so the strong rise! Nothing matters without power—Power is Eternity! KNEEL BEFORE ME! HAHAHAHA!!"

Closing the final tape softly ("History... our darkest moment. I, SumireV, always pray for you guys"), the recording faded back into present time—Codling lifting his head from his Core's archived memories with a quiet sigh: "Ah... IC truly is legendary. Lucky me I wasn't born in that era."

Chapter 86 - Ethan, Humanity's Best Regards of Its Time?

Two hundred million years ago, Universe-26 buckled beneath a colossal armada from the Deities Colony, sweeping sector by sector in unchecked conquest to harvest humanity and lesser races alike. On Day 100 of humanity's desperate insurgency, 1.3 million Celestials scoured the galaxies clean.

"We can't win—just stall them so other universes have a chance to prepare!" General Brando barked inside command while human reality-warpers fought without hope of survival, one defender firing a nuclear-electron railgun only for a Celestial to absorb the charge and vaporize him instantly.

Storming HQ, a Celestial imploded Brando's guards and lifted the General by his throat. "So this is what humans are capable of—defying divine beings? What pests."

"I may not be strong, yet we still have a surprise for you," Brando choked out. Suddenly, a supposedly extinct alien beast leaped out of hiding, clamped onto the Celestial's skull, and spat cosmic acid straight across its face—killing the Celestial on contact! Bursting outside to rampage until divine strings pinned it down, the beast bought Brando just enough time to teleport away.

Suddenly, space flickered beneath crushing gravity as a booming voice echoed across the void:

"Humanity may be weak physically, yet their mind holds infinite possibilities! And here I stand—the Mighty Host of the Human Race, come to unleash wrath in my own way!"

Out of thin air stepped Ethan, seizing a Celestial's skull and slamming it earthward hard enough to shatter the planet!

"A CORE USER! KILL HIM!" Celestials roared, hurling overlapping reality warps straight at him.

"None of you understand what humanity has achieved—we bow to no one anymore!" Ethan smirked, wrapping himself inside an Infinity Barrier that suddenly erupted outward into razor spikes, skewering charging Celestials instantly. Thirty Telekinesis Celestials and a hundred Gravity Celestials piled force onto him—only for Ethan to smile: "Core of Absolute, Gravity Base: Universal PUSH!"—flattening all 130 Celestials into thin paper sheets! Launching upward at tachyon speed, Ethan flying-kicked an entire Celestial armada into atomized dust under a galaxy-shaking shockwave as Universe-26 slammed shut in total lockdown.

Drawing a jagged sword straight out of his own spine—the Backbone of Humanity—Ethan ringed the battleground with nine flickering TON 618s to warp time against Time users, manifested the 7th Principal Ingenuity: Fallacy of Gods as a humming nuclear reactor, and fused the sword straight into it: creating the Nuclear Accelerator of Fallacy Gods Reactor!

"Core of Absolute, Creation Base: Humanity Armed to the Teeth—UNLEASH!"

Crimson-black neon weapons shrouded in unsettling dark smoke materialized across space, stripping Celestial immortality while stealing life force to heal Ethan on every hit—steadily pushing the Deities Colony backward out of Universe-26 while Ethan drifted calmly in the center lighting a cigarette. "How easy—bunch of trash, I've fought worse."

Unseen beneath a stealth cloak, a Deity lunged forward and drove an imploder sword straight into Ethan's back—only for the body to scatter into harmless sand! Appearing instantly behind the stunned killer, Ethan morphed one arm into a surgical scalpel and carved the Deity cleanly along vital energy meridians revealed by Core Vision before dropping "Core of Absolute, Gravity Base: Emergency Infinity Force!"—turning fifty kilometers into crushing gravity that splattered every hidden stealth assassin in blood! Switching seamlessly to anti-divine artillery accelerated down gravity tunnels, tornado-spinning his sword through Sentinels and Nexuses, ripping a Sentinel's tongue, throat, and jaw clean out to stuff a micro black hole inside its chest and hurl it into thousands more—detonating a 370-billion-solar-mass Phoenix A (Ethan Variant)!

Hit suddenly by Entropies and Chronoses firing concentrated beams, Ethan merely crossed his arms smiling ("Cheap tricks—love it! Creation Base: Constellation of Consume!"), dropping mechanical spiked tendrils that impaled the ambushers, devoured their bodies, energy, and memories, and ejected a fresh syringe into Ethan's palm via gravity! Jamming it straight into his arm—"Oh, that feels good, baby! Juice UP!"—he faced swarming divine legions and roared: "Core of Absolute, Thermodynamic Base: Destruction Flame—12th Level: Solar Storm Abomination!" Encased inside a blazing fireball that burst outward in scorching, venomous flame, he burned the divine armies alive in two minutes of agonizing torment.

Looking straight overhead, Ethan spotted the True Deities Armada casting a 30-minute countdown to annihilate Universe-26 completely. Checking his map to verify every human and mortal race sat safely evacuated inside his Dimension of Logic, Ethan smirked coldly.

"Core of Absolute: Entering Second Variant—Hero of Humanity!"

All of Universe-26 blazed dark crimson-red beneath surging gravity! Reshaping his Nuclear Accelerator Reactor into dual gauntlets, manifesting the 9th Principal Ingenuity as a hovering Prism, and equipping invisible "2nd Principal Ingenuity: Sacred Transient" armor (+15% passive limit break), Ethan froze space solid under "9th Principal Ingenuity, Level 8: Dark Energy Materialized!"—imploding surrounding legions before unleashing "Gravity Base: Ruler of Black Hole!" across a third of Universe-26, shattering True Deity skulls with single gauntlet blows!

When a desperate Deity invoked Higher Authority power—"Universe Rebirth!"—imploding existence itself, Ethan stood untouched inside the blank void, firing back "Core of Absolute, 7th Level: Big Bang!" from the safety of his Dimension of Logic before bursting back out at tachyon speed to kick heads cleanly off shoulders and leak "Reality Shattered!" straight across the survivors! Seizing the limbless final Deities leader ("Welcome to the human world—how does it feel watching everything shatter before you?"), Ethan severed his head with Backbone of Humanity and tossed it through a portal into his crowded Room of Trophy!

Staring across empty void afterward ("Oops—Universe is kinda gone"), Ethan raised both hands: "Core of Absolute, Creation Base—8th Level: Definitive Creation!", restoring 80% of ancient megastructures and artificial worlds, sealing Universe-26's borders inside a colossal protective Cube, returning evacuated races home, and rebuilding his Ingenuity Evergreen faction. Strolling through training grounds days later, he casually grabbed one soldier ("There's a rat among us"), punched straight through his chest, twisted out his intestines, and smashed his skull into the dirt—revealing a disguised Celestial stripped of immortality and dying in front of cheering troops.

Sitting back with coffee and a cigarette, memories flashed further back: facing seven Everest-sized Celestials (Acoustic, Theory, Destruction, Wisdom, God) across a mountain ridge; fooling them with a sand decoy to smash Wisdom's skull with a micro-black-hole baseball bat; bouncing Heavenly Energy off a Mirror to blow a sunrise hole through a neighboring peak; dropping 7th Principal Ingenuity: Fallacy of Gods as a colossal mechanical gear burning through the ozone; turning the valley into a mountain-sized industrial saw-blade factory shredding giants apart; ripping off Destruction's limbs to peel its face away with a palm black hole before greeting his friend Mary smoking atop seven corpses ("Humanity is safe again—give me two minutes and we'll head home!").

Returning to the present inside Ethan's Estate in the Realm of Absolute, Ethan wrapped up his lesson—explaining Physics Base Levels (his Thermodynamics at Level 12 and Gravity at 14–16, compared to SumireV's TON 618 at Gravity Level 6 and Phoenix A at 16.5; Singularity Rebuild was Core Level 3, not Base) while Eric teased him for repeating himself ("Lost aura, Ethan!") and reminded SumireV to seek Quartz (Schwarz, 128th user) for defense and Cosmic Suppressant training.

Bidding everyone farewell after five days ("I wish I could bring you all back to life"), SumireV stepped back out—leaving Ethan floating alone lighting a cigar, whispering a quiet secret into the air:

"Codling... is my descendant. Pretending we don't know SumireV's life outside is tough when every Core cast lets us watch her—seeing his gray eyes, his logic, that familiar aura... Maybe someday I'll let SumireV find my hidden caches and the last survivors of my era."

Chapter 87 - HIVE Death Landing

Standing on the bridge of his mothership, Codling watched the entire ZQC armada hammer the Arcadian Hive's landing zone—only for the scarred ground below to keep knitting itself back together.

"How's the situation on other fronts?" Codling asked.

"Other allies already lost over 500,000 soldiers in two hours," Nagasawa reported grimly. "Orbital bombardment kills Arcadians, yet their terrain rebuilds defenses faster than we destroy them."

"Prep orbital landing craft," Codling advised. "Deploy troops directly from Global Abomination since blocked energy signals disrupted teleportation."

"Wait—I have another idea," Devin suggested. "What if I tear open a portal in deep space right above the Hive so we drop our armies straight down?"

"Not sure if it'll hold cleanly, though losing a few thousand beating their jammer beats losing millions on a meat-grinder beach," Ethan agreed.

Codling keyed fleet comms immediately: "All ZQC units—keep pounding the primary LZ to pin their attention there! Special units and main landing waves will drop directly behind enemy lines!" Thunderous orbital, aerial, and naval salvos roared down—blasting space thick with copper blood and red mist—as Dreadnoughts and IC-engineered Behemoths (built without fallen cores, relying on antimatter cells and nuclear backup) dropped through Devin's skyward portals!

Across the sprawling Hive, allied armies bled forward along every border:

Imperium Orosian Hegemony: Already 200 kilometers inland behind 700,000,000 Divisions, endless ICBM Barrage Launchers (packing 30 two-megaton rockets per salvo), rocket-boosted machetes, and giant Auto Machines firing 25-megaton TICBMs—backed along their right flank by Radiant Guard casters weakening enemy defenses and dusting Orosian troops in Magic Dust for magical attack damage!

Centauri Enigma: 40 kilometers inland behind 250 trillion soldiers and 500 quadrillion AI humanoids running Oscillation Scorpii Emitters (+25% up to +200% short-burst stats on five-minute cooldowns), pushed ten kilometers deeper by 20-year experimented wormhole Marshall Antonia (Anomaly-B71K) despite Zone A12's terrain shifting every ten minutes.

Pivot Federation & The Empire: Flooding 500 quintillion troops alongside four Imperial Phalanx Planet War Machines (the fifth destroyed by Disputed elites) at 200,000 dead per minute—Gunther blasting crimson decay alongside Isabella's 5,000-megaton Light Blue Thunderbolts ("If you catch my heart after this war, maybe—now focus, ZQC is already here!").

Consortium Entente: Advancing 650 quintillion agile, acrobatic hit-and-run soldiers behind Consort Distortion Relay carpet bombs—sticky chemical sludge melting cells into screaming, conjoined heaps bursting with contagious acid blisters.

Over on the ZQC front, Klein maintained heavy shelling before Codling issued a chilling order—making Iflaren grab his collar furiously: "Why are you using THEM?! Those veterans aren't even human anymore—they're motherfuking killing machines!"*

"Shut up—I have a plan, and it works!" Codling snapped, shoving him back.

Suddenly, a meteoric crash rocked the landing zone as Harper stepped from the smoke raising the Arbiter Orb: "Arbiter Orb, Second Level: Decimation of Life!"—fracturing the sky in million-degree blasts! Atika hurled torn mechatronic limbs cleanly through eight mountains; Argus painted the heavens red with razor blood threads siphoning souls into his time-pausing Fallen Angel Sword; and beneath trembling earth, Jacob ("The King") raised a 9,000-meter Earth Buddha—then plunged into cracking purple-white energy to summon a continent-sized, 10-million-armed Colossal Buddha plated in Quoridium armor with back rocket thrusters, cleaving Abomination Class monsters in half ("Dakari borrowed a third of my nature power—NOTHING is worthy without POWER!").

Overhead, Estella tore reality open into a 2,900-kilometer crater while Harper unloaded Arbiter Orb: Alter Ego Version — Estella Enraged Modified (50,000 scaling 500-kiloton Cosmos Light beams per second—one of twelve components crafted for SumireV's 10th Principal Ingenuity!). Snatching Klein's microphone across battlefield loudspeakers, Estella roared defiance across every Arcadian horizon before unleashing the Almighty Division!

Seven hours later, thick, blood-humid mist drifted across western sectors—Centauri, Consortium, Orion, and Orosian troops shuddering at distant bloodcurdling screams as Argus flayed live captives to grow a towering flesh-and-blood harvesting dome four times his size, clearing roads for Tectonic Pact, Bureau Coalition, and Damien. When Harper brought over 100,000 surrendered Arcadians into camp, Estella smiled coldly ("Unfinished rage since Sigil Compact"), punched straight through one prisoner's skull to wipe gore onto Argus's flesh armor, and unleashed the Almighty Division's Groups A through Z (Annihilation, Cauterize, Destruction, Eliminate, Havoc, Kill, Obliterate, Pulverize, Ruin, Vaporize, Wipeout, Yield, Zeroth) in a slaughter heard all the way across Pivot Federation lines—while Argus notched 500 million absorbed souls onto his blade (25 million per stripe), Estella crushed crawling survivors underfoot, and Nagasawa ordered IC's Ministries of Control, Invasion, Weaponization, and Sanitization to deploy everything left!

Chapter 88 - Closing Distance

Covered in atmospheric scorch marks after tearing through distant universes, SumireV dropped into empty void Universe-28—whose Type-1 and lesser worlds offered no threat—and slipped straight inside the Realm of Absolute.

Emerging hours later to tour estates across the Realm, SumireV visited tech genius Colt (the 280th user) alongside Eric, Julian, and Ethan—stunning every predecessor with her boldest project yet: forging an unprecedented 10th Principal Ingenuity—Tesseract of Humanity (10 Levels)—built from 12 indestructible components:

Accepting Kaseng's advice to master Solemnities 1 through 9 before perfecting the 10th, SumireV plunged straight back into relentless training.

Chapter 89 - Enemy Soil

Forty-one hours into Layer 2 of the Arcadian Hive, Codling, Iflaren, Ampasy (wrapped in dangling wires and missing limbs), Dakari, and Saifuru rested inside a ZQC field ICU—saving Restoration Chambers for true emergencies while Nagasawa reported 12 trillion deaths in 24 hours and Dr. Edward and Dr. Zack deployed artificial-star-fueled rockets carrying Hyper Velocirax-Nyxvirus Arcadian Blight (HVNAB), turning Arcadian flesh into melting, imploding sludge!

Over on The Empire Front, Gunther and Isabella barely held off a 40-meter Arcadian tsunami before spotting a terrifying 500-meter wall rushing down—forcing Hohenstaufen to order a 30-minute strategic retreat! Colliding at hypervelocity to carve a 13-kilometer crater and collapsing exhausted, the pair looked up as gray-and-crimson light flickered across the sky—and Hohenstaufen dropped from warp leading The Empire's main armada, outfitted with phase-vibrating hulls allowing enemy lasers to pass harmlessly right through!

Leaping straight down among Disputed Class elites wielding his two-meter vibrating flanged mace—"Core of Dying Epoch: Bullet of Radiant!"—Hohenstaufen shattered Overseer Knight skulls like watermelons, aged 500 Overseers into dust with Permanent Death, and hurled bleeding thermobaric force spheres while cloaked Astra slipped swiftly back to warn Codling!

Leaping off his ICU cot the instant Astra reported Hohenstaufen's Core power ("That's it—we aren't staying here, let's move!"), Codling gazed out over skies painted in rival weapon glows—IC blue, Empire red, PF orange, Centauri neon, Celebus brown, Orosian maroon, Radiant Guard golden-yellow—and rallied his squad forward.

Meanwhile, deep inside Layer 2, Estella, Harper, and Damien clashed violently against an Arcadian Overlord hurling blazing Suns and Antares stars—healing scorched flesh rapidly via ice-blue/cyan-green Serum Phaser-Quantum (SP-Q) vials (3 manual-injection doses per suit granting 30-second 90% regeneration) until Damien crushed the Overlord inside a 1D fracture—only for ten more Overlords to drop around them cracking their knuckles!

Suddenly, crushing gravity slammed every Overlord flat! Reality split wide open—and SumireV stepped calmly out: "I'm back home, baby."

Squashing all ten Overlords inside giant manifested mechanical hands and sawing an invisible Overseer cleanly in half, SumireV traded sharp sibling banter with Estella ("Your acting leader summoned us!" / "Don't fu*k things up and draw a spotlight!") before rocketing straight toward Codling—leaving Estella and Jacob (sprouting crimson Trees of Death out of five impaled Abominations) pushing deeper toward encirclement.

Chapter 90 - Orosian Weaponry Might

Back on the Imperium Orosian Hegemony homeworld, 21-year-old twin rocket engineers Bro1 and Bro2 grew bored watching Hive footage and decided standard TICBMs weren't flashy enough.

Sketching wildly over sleepless hours, they tested four prototypes back-to-back: Trial 1 used pure pressure instead of TNT (1 megaton—disappointing); Trial 2 packed cluster bombs (detonated together prematurely); Trial 3 reinforced alloy casings to scatter 500-kg TNT bomblets across five kilometers cleanly; and Trial 4 packed air-ignited White Phosphorus plus long-burning Molten Metal inside every cluster—drenching an entire mountain range in raining liquid metal!

Approved instantly by the Ministry of War ("Orosian Pride—or OP!"), factories across the empire churned out 500 billion OP Rockets (plus suit-mounted mini-OPs) in two hours—choking every highway and sky lane for ten hours shipping munitions straight to the Hive!

Twenty-four hours later on the Orosian Front, sleeping Arcadians jolted awake beneath 25-meter Menckal mechs, mechanical hounds, and Orosian motherships dropping hatches loaded with 50 Type-202 OP Rockets each alongside four 800-megaton Type Apocalypse missiles! Screaming downward like Stuka dive-bombers under deafening 200-decibel shockwaves, the clusters rained burning White Phosphorus and glowing molten metal across 250 kilometers—sealing screaming Arcadians' airways solid in un-regenerating slag while Orosian generals laughed over drinks!

Frantically hailing Hive Cthulhu for backup only to hear Iflaren and SumireV slaughtering the outpost mid-call ("This place is cleared!"), the Arcadian General watched deafening OP salvos bury his mountain fortress—weeping aloud before Mach-29 rocket-thrustered elites of General Automation Unit D (Tony and his squadmates) sliced him and his captain cleanly apart!

Chapter 91 - Full Scale of Interstellar Chronicle

Arriving at Codling's Layer 2 staging camp, SumireV halted departures smoothly, returning Codling to Elite rank ("You did well—now back to work") while laying out Operation Encirclement: she would lead Iflaren, Ampasy, Astra, Saifuru, and Mikhail deep inside to shatter chokepoints and comms; Codling would herd trapped legions outward straight into Prometheus's TON 618 perimeter; and Estella would act as a giant decoy drawing enemy swarms.

Back at the ZQC Buffer Zone, Devin opened towering portals pouring every IC war department into the Hive—including Karl Siegfried (younger brother of fallen legend Herman Siegfried), roaring inside a flame-proof Quoridium Nanosuit wielding an enhanced knockoff of the 6th Principal Ingenuity! Meanwhile, Fascist Vega's entire front shattered into full retreat back to the Celebus Buffer Zone after colliding with an Arcadian Dragon four times the size of Earth!

Sweeping Sector G7 with Hyper Velocirax-Nyxvirus Arcadian Blight—designed from Zegntek's lethal concept to melt Arcadian flesh into slick oil so panicking hordes trampled each other—Iflaren suddenly ducked as a vibrating spear whistled past, turning face-to-face with his childhood friend Gunther!

Bitterly confronting Gunther over Hohenstaufen's cruel viral experiments and his father's execution for stealing a handful of rice—contrasted against IC treating him like true family—Iflaren braced to fight... only for Gunther to hurl his spear cleanly past Iflaren's shoulder, skewering an Arcadian Overlord behind him! Warning Iflaren quietly that his red Empire blood betrayed his roots to sharp observers, Gunther promised they would meet again and shot skyward leaving a crimson trail—while Iflaren launched an 80/20 HVNAB Sphere Bomb over the sector and moved onward.

Chapter 92 - Abyss Awaiting

Four hundred fifty kilometers inland inside Titan Attrition's Deeper Buffer Zone, Empire Leader Hohenstaufen briefed allied forces on their strategic breakthroughs: ZQC, Attrition Titan, and Order of North had all punched deep inland (on top of Orosian, Centauri, and GAU pushes). Yet a new complication had emerged—Pivot Federation scouts had uncovered a colossal galactic ocean inside the Hive, only 2% explored so far, named the Stellar Abyss of Arcadian. Securing naval control would require combined effort: Imperial Phalanx supplying raw alloys while The Empire pushed inland and Pivot Federation spearheaded naval warfare.

Standing among the crowd, Pivot Federation soldier Jianhao—one of eleven remaining survivors of Squad PFAF-15—whispered anxiously, "Oh God, if only I'd been assigned to the rear line, I'd be fine."

"What's wrong, Jianhao?" his friend Roslie nudged his shoulder. "Your face is pale—hide that cowardice before someone notices!"

Hours later, Squad PFAF-15 marched down to Port Federation overlooking thousands of moored warships, Roslie pointing excitedly toward the towering battleship Aurus ("My father never shuts up about that ship!") while Jianhao checked maps showing the uncharted Stellar Abyss before boarding the heavy cruiser PFS Arham. When troops grumbled about not riding a battleship, Squad Leader Higges snapped cleanly: "Shut up, kids! This isn't a joke—be grateful we're alive. Heavy cruisers see the heaviest action, so lock the fu*k in!"

Stowing gear in his cabin before stepping onto the deck where squadmates played cards, Jianhao caught the passing Captain. "Miss, when do we depart?"

"Thirty minutes," she answered briskly. "Stay sharp—whether victory or death awaits ahead, we call it Dead Ahead."

Thirty minutes later, klaxons blared across the harbor: "All troops remain below decks! Solar Rays will fire in two minutes! Repeat—clear all decks!" Scrambling below alongside his squad, Jianhao felt the ship hum as an Imperial Phalanx orbital satellite absorbed stellar radiation and fired a blinding Solar Ray straight onto the Pivot Federation armada—igniting engines and converting solar power into stored chemical reserves (leaving diesel purely as backup after solar charges drained, while battleships and carriers ran nuclear reactors). Up on deck, neon-orange energy stripes pulsed brightly along gun muzzles and boot-stripes as the fleet set sail.

An hour out across dark waves, Lizardian trooper Blackwell doubled over vomiting bright orange ("I ate a thousand eggs! I need ten kilograms of food a day or I starve to death!"), dodging Saoirse's disgusted groans ("Ewww—were you drinking Fanta?!") while Jianhao handed him a plastic bag and laughed. By sunset, purple light sank cleanly below the horizon as Jianhao shared a quiet cigarette on deck beside Higges.

At 8:00 PM sharp, thick mist swallowed the sea—until piercing alarms shattered the quiet!

Spotting hundreds of unidentified warships gliding silent through the fog at eleven o'clock glowing soft neon blue, Pivot Federation turrets swung round beneath bursting Star Shells—revealing Interstellar Chronicle banners!

"This is IC Fleet—do NOT fire! Civilians onboard! Friendly! Requesting permission to pass intel!" boomed loudspeakers across the waves.

Leaping smoothly across open water in glowing blue light, IC marines dropped thick intelligence packets onto Pivot Federation decks—one handing a sheet straight to Jianhao—before bounding back aboard vessels sporting spinning overhead sonar discs and dual-rocket Destroyers, sounding deep horns before vanishing silently into stealth!

Glancing down, Jianhao read the brief: "From IC Update and Status Department: Kikel race survivors spotted along coastal beaches—rescue on sight and provide medical aid. Orosian Naval Force encountered a gigantic nocturnal sea monster—stay vigilant and kill on sight."

Before Jianhao could blink, the ocean turned pitch black beneath howling hurricane winds and crackling lightning! Rising straight ahead through the storm towered a monstrous silhouette—six glowing arms, rows of razor-sharp teeth, and blazing yellow light surging inside its jaws!

Alarms screamed as a Destructor Beam tore through several escort ships while legions of Arcadians rained straight out of storm clouds onto the decks! Every Pivot Federation warship roared back simultaneously with 450mm armor-piercing broadsides, driving the howling leviathan underwater—while deck chaos erupted!

"Everyone ENGAGE!" Higges roared, leaping onto charging Arcadians.

Igniting his Crude Oil ability, Jianhao drowned attackers in boiling, sticky flames, liquefied his body to send charging troops skidding into adhesive puddles while massive Lizardian Blackwell stomped skulls flat, and nearly took a Beast's Destructor Beam point-blank before Saoirse snapped open a portal dropping him safely onto the opposite deck ("I'll support—keep fighting!"). Engaging his suit's glowing neon-orange one-meter forearm plasma blades, Jianhao carved cleanly through incoming raiders—until the six-armed sea monster burst up at three o'clock priming another beam!

BOOOOM!

A shattering thunderclap tore across the sea as glowing neon-orange Double Laceration Armor-Piercing Shells (DLAP) from Battleship Aurus blasted one of the monster's arms clean off, shredding its hide with secondary lacerations! Diving screaming beneath Star Shells while Federation submarines engaged it below, the monster thrashed wildly until sonic booms tore overhead—Pivot Federation Sentinels and Archons plummeting straight into the waves amid blinding underwater flashes!

Surfacing moments later hoisting the monster's severed head ("Mutated Kikel victims—sensitive to sunlight, keep UV Lights blazing at night!"), a Sentinel crystallized the trophy and shattered it into dust before shooting skyward. As the storm cleared peacefully, Jianhao lit a cigar beside Blackwell: "Going to be a long battle—good luck."

Chapter 93 - Opening Front For Naval Warfare

Three days after their first clash with the sea monster, the Pivot Federation armada steamed northwest toward suspected enemy territory. Sleeping in his bunk, Jianhao woke to Saoirse shaking his shoulder ("Jian, come see this!"), stepping behind a tense Higges holding down his hat toward the upper hatch. Jammed shut with corroded residue, the heavy door gave way easily under Blackwell's muscle—releasing a gagging stench of rot and crimson stains across the deck.

Checking his wrist gauge, Jianhao saw warnings for Hyper Velocirax-Nyxvirus Arcadian Blight—harmless to non-Arcadians, lethal to the enemy. Peering over the railing, they stared across blood-red waves choked with shattered Arcadian hulls and battered Interstellar Chronicle Naval Vessels (ICNV). Entering a towering bank of dark crimson mist cutting sunlight down to 30%, crews snapped on floodlights—passing capsized hulls scraping eerily from trapped survivors inside, stripped skeletons clinging to keels, and horrified Arcadians sprinting across floating wreckage pursued by butterfly-rib-winged conjoined infected!

"I always thought IC's bioweapon rumors were just a myth..." Blackwell whispered grimly while Jianhao clenched his fists fighting down nausea.

Suddenly, clean loudspeakers cut through the fog: "This is ICNV! Friendly—Allies!"

Returning smoothly to his flagship—super-heavy battleship ICNV Pantera, bristling with six quadruple-barrel turrets, heavy AA, and torpedo batteries—Aziz triggered a thunderous sonar ping that parted the crimson mist outright, unveiling thousands of ruined Arcadian wrecks as PFAF-15 stared in awe at IC's relentless, self-healing floating fortresses.

"General Quarters!" Commander John barked. "Bring guns to bear on the shoreline, starboard side, bearing 090! Load Super Laceration Explosive Shells!"

"FIRE!" Commander Aziz commanded.

The sea trembled violently beneath rolling broadsides! "Deities or fakes—can you survive humanity's firepower?"

CRASH!

Core of Absolute, 3rd Level: Singularity Rebuild—UNLEASH!"

In the old days humans bled over labels, yet faith gives our spirits real armor against spiritual attacks." Nagasawa nodded thoughtfully before returning to work.

Chapter 100 - Utopia of IC

Free schools, colleges, and universities with custom entry courses! Across every IC city, crime, unemployment, poverty, and illness sit cleanly at 0.00%!"

"Lonely or insecure? Shapeshifting AI companions match any dream look, real IC citizens love genuinely, and Casket V7 fixes body or fertility issues—leaving infidelity at 0.00%! Chapter 101 - Aegis Of Last Embers

Inside Radiant Guard, Queen Odette summoned ancient Deity Nergal to smash the Arcadian flank beside the Orosian Front.
