On the Northern Front, Spectre Core user Vlad—one of the 13 Aegis of Last Embers, wielding the Core of Ruin—led 984 trillion (swelling to quadrillions) Order of North Cavalry, 1st–5th Stal' Divisions, and Queen Kira's 71st Krasny Soldat Division behind Parliament Abstract's Frozo Satellites (freezing battlefields so cavalry skated at Mach 5) and Sovereign of Eclipse healers. Unleashing "Core of Ruin: Sentient Bloom!"—white True-Damage fields piercing shields and spare lives to atomize enemies—Vlad raised the Sickle and Hammer skyward with a thunderous "URA!" toward Layer 2.

Meanwhile, in a GAU bar, Overseer Zephyrus—Aegis wielder of the Core of Volatile (copying any Universe-26 Core ability for two-minute bursts)—casually killed a drunken challenger with a tossed glass before Admiral Adam paged him directly to SumireV's Aegis reunion inside Layer 2.

Meeting beside SumireV, Vlad, Nergal, Hohenstaufen, Thuban, and Mona (Leader of Consortium Entente / Celebus Alliance, Protector of Hybrid Human Beasts), Zephyrus heard SumireV expose the Arcadians' Layer 6 ritual meant to bypass Ethan's Cube and summon True Deities or Higher Authorities! Locking in a two-month battle plan—clear Layers 1–4 conventionally, pin Layer 6 inside Layer 5 with elites, have Parliament Abstract flash-freeze cleared layers, keep Nergal feeding energy, and unleash Vlad's Northern Vision with total Core demolition as backup—Zephyrus returned to GAU lines (where barely 5% of 16 million Artificial Celestials worked), tested Volatile: Core of Absolute and Core of Flamer: Serpent of Flame (90 MT TNT), and nearly brawled Dieter in Layer 2 before Gunther intervened.

Suddenly, emergency alerts flooded Zephyrus's screen from Planets G-001 through G-005! Contacting Admiral Adam, he learned GAU's Ministry of Health had sprayed experimental Velocirax-Nyxvirus over civilian cities claiming it was a preventative vaccine while officials wore gas masks—spawning Chameleon-camo infected and acid-spitting Alpha Variants at 20,000 infections per second! Racing home at lightspeed with summoned Commonwealth, PA, and SoE Sigil Compact veterans—confirming G-001 to G-003 completely lost (G-004 at 20% clean; G-005 holding 64% behind nuclear scorched-earth walls while evacuating two immune carriers to orbit)—Zephyrus flew to glass G-001.

At that exact instant, eleven out of 549 GAU planets erupted into civil war as Facundo (Core of Tectonic, leading Movement Liberation) stormed GAU HQ clashing swords with Admirals Raymond and Syazwan—while Fascist Vega (who armed the rebels) slammed 200 trillion troops across GAU's northern border (Operation Wing) and Orion-TW3 drove 1.2 quadrillion vengeful troops straight into the southwest!

Chapter 102 - Protector of Humanity Augmentation

Days before her return, inside the Realm of Absolute, SumireV studied Tanya's (291st) list of viable sub-Level-5 resurrection candidates (Ronan, Ava, Avalon, Gina, Stuart, Michael)—choosing Ekaterina (285th user, Level 4.5, Acoustic Specialty), backed by Revenant's arrival from Cluster Creed, two spare Cores, Mao, and IC's TON 618 reactor. Prompted by Eleanor (93rd), with Ethan guiding her and predecessors nervously clutching custom Solemnities, SumireV pushed past Level 5 into her Core Variant: "Core of Absolute, Second Variant: Radiant Grim" (+40% permanent baseline boost)—hurling a 3-by-2-meter Cross at Mach 15 cleanly through a peak while Ekaterina gleefully blasted simulated planets with "Symphony of Destruction" and collapsed 13 dimensions!

Back in real space, Dr. Pedro unveiled the floating 2-meter-tall Cross of IC—forged from Existential Quoridium smelted with Hallowed Drops, fitted with an AI buddy and inspired by Han of Sigil Compact: packing Black Hole Kunai, Velocirax-Nyxvirus Transient Enhanced syringes, 5%-capped Solemnity casts, Estella-team Invasive Web sticky foam grenades, 3D-printed Railguns/miniguns/melee/turrets, 60% Radical Collapse, 9–14 Velocirax-Nyxvirus Cluster Bombs, Hallowed 7th Solemnity Aura (-20% enemy energy + +10% damage vs. Seven Sins), and a 100-meter field giving 5%/s healing plus toggleable Agility, Strength, Stealth, Cosmic, Planet, Deity Buster, and Protector of Humanity (+15% all stats) modes.

Portaling to the IC War Planet—checking Ampasy dropping core-sinking weights, Saifuru, and Crown's upgraded Proton Cannon—SumireV learned of GAU's viral crisis and Orion/Vega's invasion, choosing to stay focused on Arcadians while leaving Tectonic Pact and Bureau Coalition to lightly assist Orion and granting Estella permission to deploy Sigil Compact Veterans armed with sealed ancestral weapons inside Almighty Variant suits.

Unsealing Casket 777 inside a hidden War Planet bunker, SumireV awakened Astra's Oblivion comrade Ares clad in Serum Phaser-777. Testing "Authorization of Calamity: Destructor of Twilight!", Ares recounted Cataclysm (parent force of Havoc, Destructor, and Calamity), Oblivion ranks (Letheans, Primordial Trinitas, royal Null Order), and how a dying Sun fused six Oblivion factions into him as Eclipse Oblivion (Annular & Total Eclipse eyes, Light Speed Generation 2, and a mysterious Key on his palm) before an apocalyptic blast flung him to Universe-26. Shipping the War Planet, Second Behemoth, and ICCF Divisions 1 & 2 into the Hive, SumireV reunited with Codling, Klein, and recovering Zero at the ZQC Buffer Zone—introducing Ares and Gamma Ray Burst powerhouse Duncan to guard a deep-space Revival Platform alongside Joshua's Hallowed Holy Divisions 1–7, Estella's Human Variant Almighty Veterans, and MiRi, Prometheus, and Mikhail (victorious over the Arcadian Dragon)—planting IC's flag to ignite every vessel and Global Abomination Hydra (Gen 2) across space in blazing dark neon blue!

Across Layer 2, IC tore forward relentlessly: Sky, Ampasy, Codling, Saifuru, and Iflaren crushed an Arcadian Halo to rescue an Orion captain; Crown climbed atop Jacob's 1,000-meter Quoridium Buddha for a combined "Almighty Push!" before grief ignited Jacob's "Forbidden Scroll: Armageddon Resurgence!"—sprouting continent-sized multi-armed Buddhas, Pollinators of Death, and a crimson Tree of Death turning infected Arcadians into trees of blood; Estella and Dieter smashed Demi-God fists beside Pick Mattocks; at the Revival Platform, Duncan pulverized Kikel raiders before Ares erupted from a Destructor Cocoon as Eldritch of Oblivion (Cataclysm Blade arm, multi-mouthed Havoc Sphere mask, split Annular-eye chest, four charred skeleton arms)—wiping the ambush fleet inside a terrifying apocalyptic illusion of Universe-26 collapsing; deep in space, comforted by visions of his late father, Mao ignited Core of Serenity to supercharge IC's entire frontline; and surging onto Titan Attrition's Eastern Layer 2, the Entropy-tier New Big Seven Sentinels (Omni Absorber, Redundancy Buffer, Dark Structure Cosmo [DsC], Valastro Memphis, Judah Fire, Celestial's Ark Break, and Revelation Olympia) marveled at SumireV's 9-billion-square-kilometer molten battle scar before uniting with Pivot Federation's Core user and 2 septillion troops for the push toward Layer 3.

Chapter 103 - Unfairness & Ignorance

Far from the warfronts, inside Orion-TW3 territory, an overweight straight-A tech student named Remington sat eating in his college cafeteria, quietly hoping he wouldn't get drafted for Orion's war against the GAU.

Across the room, six swaggering school bullies cornered four terrified girls beside a table. "Hey beautiful, wanna come back to my place tonight? I've got something you'll like~"

Looking around the cafeteria, Remington counted over eighty classmates—every single one staring down at their trays, whispering and hiding in blissful ignorance just to stay safe.

"Eighty of us and six of them," Remington murmured bitterly. "If nobody steps up now, this keeps repeating generation after generation. Better to stand up as a creepy otaku than stay silent!"

Stepping between them, Remington spoke loudly: "Hey! Stop! You're hurting those girls!"

"Oh, look—the creepy otaku wants to play hero?" the leader sneered. "Wanna get punched?"

"Better a creepy otaku than a loser harassing innocent girls just to brag about body counts," Remington shot back. "Even if nobody else stands up, everyone here knows what you're doing is WRONG!"

Grabbing Remington's collar, the leader drove a fist into his gut while all six piled on—Remington using his heavy frame to plow straight forward, hitting record on his phone, and shouting "RUN, GIRLS!" before barreling straight toward the Discipline Office while the bullies bolted! Filing his footage and report under the school's "Say No to Bullying" pledge, Remington walked back out smiling slightly—only to freeze at cold stares and students ducking away down every hall.

The next morning confirmed his worst dread: classmates snickered behind their hands, delinquents glared past the classroom door, Discipline offered empty words just to bury the scandal, and older allies were deployed off-world. The instant break bell rang and every classmate rushed out leaving him packing alone, twelve armed bullies stepped inside and locked the door.

"I knew this was coming," Remington murmured calmly, strapping his backpack tight.

Fighting with cold precision—using pure momentum, bone-crushing knee drops onto pinned chests, and hurling a knife-wielder straight out a glass window—Remington left all twelve bullies groaning with shattered bones across the floor thirty minutes later, only for Discipline officers to burst in and instantly blame Remington.

Sitting alone after school on a bench playing mobile games—mocked across group chats as "a creepy gooner otaku wannabe hero"—Remington glanced up as a quiet, seemingly emotionless girl stopped beside him.

"Hello, Remington. I'm Penelope. I saw everything you did," she said softly. "You moved my humanity—something that's been numb for a long time."

Inviting her to sit, Remington learned Penelope had once fought cyberbullying alone to protect girls until the same rotten system abandoned her too. Bonding over being cast out by hollow peers devoid of empathy, Remington smiled and invited her home for his mother's cooking—Penelope accepting immediately from her nearby flat.

Sitting in Remington's bedroom later staring at a dead-end future stripped of school and reputation, Penelope whispered, "If there's a goal, there's a path..."

"Sure—except when there's no path left ahead," Remington sighed.

"When there's no path... do you turn around, or dig straight through the wall?"

Suddenly, both froze—eyes lighting up at the exact same thought. Orion-TW3 held a close alliance with Tectonic Pact, Bureau Coalition, and Interstellar Chronicle!

"THE IC!" Remington jumped to his feet.

"YES—the land of the future, hope, and freedom!" Penelope cheered beside him.

Plotting immediately to sharpen their IQ and combat skills, drop their worthless school for good, and email Penelope's off-world parents from Remington's desktop, the pair paused as Remington's mother called them down for dinner—Remington flashing Penelope a confident grin before heading downstairs:

"Let's go—because we finally know what awaits people like us."
